{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CdUTdnVX5o1ZYVssWVzDY","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01BKKhAV9PUwV5ShyHfgJh5z","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/security-goal-002/2026-07-28T122814Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/src/LendingPool.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity 0.8.28;\n\nimport {IERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol\";\nimport {IERC20Metadata} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/extensions/IERC20Metadata.sol\";\nimport {SafeERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol\";\nimport {ReentrancyGuard} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/ReentrancyGuard.sol\";\nimport {Pausable} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/Pausable.sol\";\nimport {Ownable, Ownable2Step} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable2Step.sol\";\nimport {Math} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/math/Math.sol\";\n\nimport {IAggregatorV3} from \"./interfaces/IAggregatorV3.sol\";\n\n/// @title LendingPool\n/// @notice Single-pair borrowing market: lock `collateralToken` (WETH), borrow `debtToken` (USDC)\n///         against it up to 70% LTV. Debt accrues a flat annual rate. Positions above the 85%\n///         liquidation threshold can be partially closed by anyone for a 5% collateral bonus.\n/// @dev    Lending liquidity is supplied by the operator (`fund`), not by public depositors —\n///         there are no supply-side shares. See NOTES.md for the full trust model.\ncontract LendingPool is Ownable2Step, Pausable, ReentrancyGuard {\n    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                                CONSTANTS                               */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    uint256 public constant BPS = 10_000;\n    /// @dev Interest index precision (27 decimals), Aave-style.\n    uint256 public constant RAY = 1e27;\n    uint256 public constant SECONDS_PER_YEAR = 365 days;\n    /// @dev All USD values are carried internally with 18 decimals.\n    uint256 internal constant USD_UNIT = 1e18;\n\n    /// @notice Max borrow power: debt value may not exceed 70% of collateral value.\n    uint256 public constant MAX_LTV_BPS = 7_000;\n    /// @notice A position is liquidatable once debt value exceeds 85% of collateral value.\n    uint256 public constant LIQUIDATION_THRESHOLD_BPS = 8_500;\n    /// @notice Liquidator seizes collateral worth repaid debt + 5%.\n    uint256 public constant LIQUIDATION_BONUS_BPS = 500;\n    /// @notice At most 50% of a position's debt can be repaid in a single liquidation.\n    uint256 public constant MAX_CLOSE_FACTOR_BPS = 5_000;\n\n    /// @notice Hard ceiling on the owner-settable borrow rate (50% APR).\n    uint256 public constant MAX_BORROW_RATE_BPS = 5_000;\n    /// @notice Bounds on the owner-settable oracle staleness windows.\n    uint256 public constant MIN_STALENESS_WINDOW = 10 minutes;\n    uint256 public constant MAX_STALENESS_WINDOW = 2 days;\n    /// @notice Bound on the owner-settable dust floor (in debt-token units).\n    uint256 public constant MAX_MIN_DEBT = 100_000;\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                               IMMUTABLES                               */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @notice Collateral asset (WETH on mainnet).\n    IERC20 public immutable collateralToken;\n    /// @notice Borrowable asset (USDC on mainnet).\n    IERC20 public immutable debtToken;\n    /// @dev 10 ** decimals() of each token, read at construction. Never hardcode 1e18.\n    uint256 public immutable collateralUnit;\n    uint256 public immutable debtUnit;\n\n    /// @notice Chainlink feed for collateral, quoted in USD (ETH/USD).\n    IAggregatorV3 public immutable collateralFeed;\n    /// @notice Chainlink feed for the borrowed asset, quoted in USD (USDC/USD).\n    IAggregatorV3 public immutable debtFeed;\n    /// @dev Multiplier that lifts each feed's answer to 18 decimals.\n    uint256 internal immutable collateralFeedScale;\n    uint256 internal immutable debtFeedScale;\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                                 STORAGE                                */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @notice Flat annual borrow rate in basis points.\n    uint256 public borrowRateBps;\n    /// @notice Cumulative interest index, RAY-scaled. Monotonically non-decreasing.\n    uint256 public borrowIndex;\n    /// @notice Timestamp the index was last brought up to date.\n    uint256 public lastAccrualTime;\n    /// @notice Sum of all index-scaled debt.\n    uint256 public totalScaledDebt;\n\n    /// @notice Minimum position debt, in debt-token units. Keeps positions worth liquidating.\n    uint256 public minDebt;\n    /// @notice Max age of a collateral-feed answer before it is rejected.\n    uint256 public collateralFeedStaleAfter;\n    /// @notice Max age of a debt-feed answer before it is rejected.\n    uint256 public debtFeedStaleAfter;\n\n    /// @notice Collateral balance per borrower, in collateral-token units.\n    mapping(address account => uint256 amount) public collateralOf;\n    /// @notice Index-scaled debt per borrower. Actual debt = scaled * borrowIndex / RAY.\n    mapping(address account => uint256 scaled) public scaledDebtOf;\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                                  EVENTS                                */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    event CollateralDeposited(address indexed account, address indexed payer, uint256 amount);\n    event CollateralWithdrawn(address indexed account, address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n    event Borrowed(address indexed account, address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n    event Repaid(address indexed account, address indexed payer, uint256 amount);\n    event Liquidated(\n        address indexed borrower,\n        address indexed liquidator,\n        uint256 debtRepaid,\n        uint256 collateralSeized,\n        uint256 remainingDebt\n    );\n    /// @dev Emitted when a liquidation exhausts the borrower's collateral and leaves debt behind.\n    event BadDebt(address indexed borrower, uint256 remainingDebt);\n    event Accrued(uint256 borrowIndex, uint256 timestamp);\n    event BorrowRateSet(uint256 oldRateBps, uint256 newRateBps);\n    event MinDebtSet(uint256 oldMinDebt, uint256 newMinDebt);\n    event StalenessWindowsSet(uint256 collateralFeedStaleAfter, uint256 debtFeedStaleAfter);\n    event ReserveFunded(address indexed from, uint256 amount);\n    event ReserveWithdrawn(address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                                  ERRORS                                */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    error ZeroAddress();\n    error ZeroAmount();\n    error InvalidDecimals();\n    error InvalidParameter();\n    error PriceNotPositive();\n    error PriceStale(uint256 updatedAt, uint256 maxAge);\n    error PriceRoundIncomplete();\n    error InsufficientCollateral();\n    error ExceedsBorrowPower(uint256 debtValue, uint256 maxDebtValue);\n    error InsufficientLiquidity(uint256 requested, uint256 available);\n    error NoDebt();\n    error DebtBelowMinimum(uint256 debt, uint256 minDebt);\n    error PositionHealthy(uint256 debtValue, uint256 liquidationValue);\n    error NothingToLiquidate();\n    error SlippageExceeded(uint256 seized, uint256 minSeized);\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                              CONSTRUCTION                              */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @param owner_ Operator address. Must be a multisig/timelock on mainnet.\n    /// @param collateralToken_ WETH.\n    /// @param debtToken_ USDC.\n    /// @param collateralFeed_ Chainlink ETH/USD.\n    /// @param debtFeed_ Chainlink USDC/USD.\n    /// @param borrowRateBps_ Flat annual rate, in bps.\n    /// @param minDebt_ Dust floor in debt-token units (e.g. 100e6 = 100 USDC).\n    /// @param collateralFeedStaleAfter_ Staleness window for the collateral feed.\n    /// @param debtFeedStaleAfter_ Staleness window for the debt feed.\n    constructor(\n        address owner_,\n        address collateralToken_,\n        address debtToken_,\n        address collateralFeed_,\n        address debtFeed_,\n        uint256 borrowRateBps_,\n        uint256 minDebt_,\n        uint256 collateralFeedStaleAfter_,\n        uint256 debtFeedStaleAfter_\n    ) Ownable(owner_) {\n        if (\n            owner_ == address(0) || collateralToken_ == address(0) || debtToken_ == address(0)\n                || collateralFeed_ == address(0) || debtFeed_ == address(0)\n        ) revert ZeroAddress();\n        if (collateralToken_ == debtToken_) revert InvalidParameter();\n\n        collateralToken = IERC20(collateralToken_);\n        debtToken = IERC20(debtToken_);\n\n        // Decimals are read from the tokens, never assumed. USDC is 6, WETH is 18.\n        uint8 colDec = IERC20Metadata(collateralToken_).decimals();\n        uint8 debtDec = IERC20Metadata(debtToken_).decimals();\n        if (colDec > 18 || debtDec > 18) revert InvalidDecimals();\n        collateralUnit = 10 ** colDec;\n        debtUnit = 10 ** debtDec;\n\n        collateralFeed = IAggregatorV3(collateralFeed_);\n        debtFeed = IAggregatorV3(debtFeed_);\n        uint8 colFeedDec = IAggregatorV3(collateralFeed_).decimals();\n        uint8 debtFeedDec = IAggregatorV3(debtFeed_).decimals();\n        if (colFeedDec > 18 || debtFeedDec > 18) revert InvalidDecimals();\n        collateralFeedScale = 10 ** (18 - colFeedDec);\n        debtFeedScale = 10 ** (18 - debtFeedDec);\n\n        if (borrowRateBps_ > MAX_BORROW_RATE_BPS) revert InvalidParameter();\n        if (minDebt_ == 0 || minDebt_ > MAX_MIN_DEBT * debtUnit) revert InvalidParameter();\n        _validateStaleness(collateralFeedStaleAfter_, debtFeedStaleAfter_);\n\n        borrowRateBps = borrowRateBps_;\n        minDebt = minDebt_;\n        collateralFeedStaleAfter = collateralFeedStaleAfter_;\n        debtFeedStaleAfter = debtFeedStaleAfter_;\n\n        borrowIndex = RAY;\n        lastAccrualTime = block.timestamp;\n\n        emit BorrowRateSet(0, borrowRateBps_);\n        emit MinDebtSet(0, minDebt_);\n        emit StalenessWindowsSet(collateralFeedStaleAfter_, debtFeedStaleAfter_);\n    }\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                           INTEREST ACCRUAL                             */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @notice Brings the borrow index up to the current block. Callable by anyone.\n    /// @dev Every state-changing entry point calls this first, so the index is never stale\n    ///      when health is evaluated.\n    function accrue() public {\n        uint256 elapsed = block.timestamp - lastAccrualTime;\n        if (elapsed == 0) return;\n        lastAccrualTime = block.timestamp;\n\n        uint256 rate = borrowRateBps;\n        // No debt outstanding, or a zero rate: nothing to compound, but the clock still moves\n        // forward so idle time can never be charged retroactively.\n        if (rate == 0 || totalScaledDebt == 0) return;\n\n        uint256 newIndex = _indexAt(borrowIndex, rate, elapsed);\n        borrowIndex = newIndex;\n        emit Accrued(newIndex, block.timestamp);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Simple interest applied over `elapsed`, compounded across accruals.\n    function _indexAt(uint256 index, uint256 rate, uint256 elapsed) internal pure returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 growth = RAY + Math.mulDiv(RAY * rate, elapsed, BPS * SECONDS_PER_YEAR);\n        return Math.mulDiv(index, growth, RAY);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Borrow index as it would be after accruing to the current block.\n    function currentBorrowIndex() public view returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 elapsed = block.timestamp - lastAccrualTime;\n        uint256 rate = borrowRateBps;\n        if (elapsed == 0 || rate == 0 || totalScaledDebt == 0) return borrowIndex;\n        return _indexAt(borrowIndex, rate, elapsed);\n    }\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                            BORROWER ACTIONS                            */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @notice Lock collateral for `onBehalfOf`. Tokens are pulled from `msg.sender`.\n    /// @dev Credits the balance actually received, so a fee-on-transfer collateral token\n    ///      could never be credited for more than the pool holds.\n    function depositCollateral(uint256 amount, address onBehalfOf) external nonReentrant whenNotPaused {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (onBehalfOf == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        accrue();\n\n        uint256 balanceBefore = collateralToken.balanceOf(address(this));\n        collateralToken.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n        uint256 received = collateralToken.balanceOf(address(this)) - balanceBefore;\n        if (received == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n        collateralOf[onBehalfOf] += received;\n        emit CollateralDeposited(onBehalfOf, msg.sender, received);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Withdraw collateral. The position must stay within the 70% LTV afterwards.\n    function withdrawCollateral(uint256 amount, address to) external nonReentrant whenNotPaused {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (to == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        accrue();\n\n        uint256 balance = collateralOf[msg.sender];\n        if (amount > balance) revert InsufficientCollateral();\n\n        // Effects before the external transfer (CEI).\n        uint256 remaining = balance - amount;\n        collateralOf[msg.sender] = remaining;\n        _requireWithinBorrowPower(msg.sender, remaining);\n\n        collateralToken.safeTransfer(to, amount);\n        emit CollateralWithdrawn(msg.sender, to, amount);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Borrow `amount` of the debt token against already-locked collateral.\n    function borrow(uint256 amount, address to) external nonReentrant whenNotPaused {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (to == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        accrue();\n\n        uint256 available = debtToken.balanceOf(address(this));\n        if (amount > available) revert InsufficientLiquidity(amount, available);\n\n        // Round the borrower's scaled debt up: rounding always favours the pool.\n        uint256 scaledDelta = Math.mulDiv(amount, RAY, borrowIndex, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n        scaledDebtOf[msg.sender] += scaledDelta;\n        totalScaledDebt += scaledDelta;\n\n        uint256 newDebt = _debtOf(msg.sender, borrowIndex);\n        if (newDebt < minDebt) revert DebtBelowMinimum(newDebt, minDebt);\n        _requireWithinBorrowPower(msg.sender, collateralOf[msg.sender]);\n\n        debtToken.safeTransfer(to, amount);\n        emit Borrowed(msg.sender, to, amount);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Repay debt for `onBehalfOf`. Pass `type(uint256).max` to clear the position.\n    /// @dev Intentionally callable while paused so borrowers can always de-risk.\n    /// @return repaid The debt-token amount actually pulled from `msg.sender`.\n    function repay(uint256 amount, address onBehalfOf) external nonReentrant returns (uint256 repaid) {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (onBehalfOf == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        accrue();\n\n        uint256 index = borrowIndex;\n        uint256 debt = _debtOf(onBehalfOf, index);\n        if (debt == 0) revert NoDebt();\n\n        repaid = amount > debt ? debt : amount;\n\n        uint256 scaledDelta;\n        if (repaid == debt) {\n            // Full repayment: burn the exact scaled balance so no dust can survive rounding.\n            scaledDelta = scaledDebtOf[onBehalfOf];\n        } else {\n            // Round the debt reduction down: rounding always favours the pool.\n            scaledDelta = Math.mulDiv(repaid, RAY, index, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        }\n\n        uint256 remainingScaled = scaledDebtOf[onBehalfOf] - scaledDelta;\n        scaledDebtOf[onBehalfOf] = remainingScaled;\n        totalScaledDebt -= scaledDelta;\n\n        uint256 remainingDebt = _debtOf(onBehalfOf, index);\n        if (remainingDebt != 0 && remainingDebt < minDebt) revert DebtBelowMinimum(remainingDebt, minDebt);\n\n        debtToken.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), repaid);\n        emit Repaid(onBehalfOf, msg.sender, repaid);\n    }\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                              LIQUIDATION                               */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @notice Repay part of an unhealthy position's debt and seize the matching collateral\n    ///         plus a 5% bonus. Callable by anyone.\n    /// @param borrower Position to liquidate.\n    /// @param repayAmount Debt tokens the caller is willing to repay; capped at the close factor.\n    /// @param minCollateralOut Slippage guard — revert if less collateral than this is seized.\n    /// @return repaid Debt tokens actually pulled from the caller.\n    /// @return seized Collateral tokens sent to the caller.\n    function liquidate(address borrower, uint256 repayAmount, uint256 minCollateralOut)\n        external\n        nonReentrant\n        whenNotPaused\n        returns (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized)\n    {\n        if (borrower == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        if (repayAmount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        accrue();\n\n        uint256 index = borrowIndex;\n        uint256 debt = _debtOf(borrower, index);\n        if (debt == 0) revert NoDebt();\n\n        uint256 collateral = collateralOf[borrower];\n        uint256 collateralValue = _collateralValueUsd(collateral);\n        uint256 debtValue = _debtValueUsd(debt, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n        uint256 liquidationValue = Math.mulDiv(collateralValue, LIQUIDATION_THRESHOLD_BPS, BPS);\n        if (debtValue <= liquidationValue) revert PositionHealthy(debtValue, liquidationValue);\n\n        bool seizedAll;\n        (repaid, seized, seizedAll) = _computeLiquidation(debt, collateral, repayAmount);\n        if (repaid == 0 || seized == 0) revert NothingToLiquidate();\n        if (seized < minCollateralOut) revert SlippageExceeded(seized, minCollateralOut);\n\n        uint256 scaledDelta;\n        if (repaid == debt) {\n            scaledDelta = scaledDebtOf[borrower];\n        } else {\n            scaledDelta = Math.mulDiv(repaid, RAY, index, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        }\n\n        // Effects.\n        scaledDebtOf[borrower] -= scaledDelta;\n        totalScaledDebt -= scaledDelta;\n        collateralOf[borrower] = collateral - seized;\n\n        uint256 remainingDebt = _debtOf(borrower, index);\n        // A position left with dust debt is not worth liquidating later; only allow it when the\n        // collateral is exhausted (nothing more a liquidator could take anyway).\n        if (!seizedAll && remainingDebt != 0 && remainingDebt < minDebt) {\n            revert DebtBelowMinimum(remainingDebt, minDebt);\n        }\n\n        // Interactions.\n        debtToken.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), repaid);\n        collateralToken.safeTransfer(msg.sender, seized);\n\n        emit Liquidated(borrower, msg.sender, repaid, seized, remainingDebt);\n        if (seizedAll && remainingDebt != 0) emit BadDebt(borrower, remainingDebt);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Off-chain helper: what a liquidation of `borrower` for `repayAmount` would do.\n    /// @dev Reverts with the same errors as `liquidate` for a healthy or debt-free position.\n    function previewLiquidation(address borrower, uint256 repayAmount)\n        external\n        view\n        returns (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized)\n    {\n        uint256 index = currentBorrowIndex();\n        uint256 debt = _debtOf(borrower, index);\n        if (debt == 0) revert NoDebt();\n\n        uint256 collateral = collateralOf[borrower];\n        uint256 debtValue = _debtValueUsd(debt, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n        uint256 liquidationValue = Math.mulDiv(_collateralValueUsd(collateral), LIQUIDATION_THRESHOLD_BPS, BPS);\n        if (debtValue <= liquidationValue) revert PositionHealthy(debtValue, liquidationValue);\n\n        (repaid, seized,) = _computeLiquidation(debt, collateral, repayAmount);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Splits liquidation sizing out of `liquidate` so the preview cannot drift from it.\n    function _computeLiquidation(uint256 debt, uint256 collateral, uint256 requestedRepay)\n        internal\n        view\n        returns (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized, bool seizedAll)\n    {\n        uint256 maxRepay = Math.mulDiv(debt, MAX_CLOSE_FACTOR_BPS, BPS);\n        // Never leave a sub-minimum remainder that no one would bother liquidating.\n        if (debt - maxRepay < minDebt) maxRepay = debt;\n\n        repaid = requestedRepay > maxRepay ? maxRepay : requestedRepay;\n        if (repaid == 0) return (0, 0, false);\n\n        seized = _seizeForRepay(repaid);\n        if (seized >= collateral) {\n            // Underwater position: hand over what is left and shrink the repayment to match,\n            // so the liquidator still earns the bonus on what they actually get.\n            seized = collateral;\n            seizedAll = true;\n            repaid = _repayForSeize(collateral);\n            if (repaid > debt) repaid = debt;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Collateral owed to a liquidator for repaying `repayAmount`, including the bonus.\n    ///      Rounds down: the pool never over-pays a liquidator on rounding.\n    function _seizeForRepay(uint256 repayAmount) internal view returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 valueUsd = _debtValueUsd(repayAmount, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        uint256 withBonus = Math.mulDiv(valueUsd, BPS + LIQUIDATION_BONUS_BPS, BPS);\n        return _collateralForValueUsd(withBonus);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Inverse of `_seizeForRepay`: debt covered by seizing exactly `collateralAmount`.\n    function _repayForSeize(uint256 collateralAmount) internal view returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 valueUsd = _collateralValueUsd(collateralAmount);\n        uint256 withoutBonus = Math.mulDiv(valueUsd, BPS, BPS + LIQUIDATION_BONUS_BPS);\n        return _debtForValueUsd(withoutBonus);\n    }\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                                 PRICING                                */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @notice Collateral price in USD, 18 decimals.\n    function collateralPrice() public view returns (uint256) {\n        return _readFeed(collateralFeed, collateralFeedScale, collateralFeedStaleAfter);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Debt-token price in USD, 18 decimals. Not assumed to be exactly $1.\n    function debtPrice() public view returns (uint256) {\n        return _readFeed(debtFeed, debtFeedScale, debtFeedStaleAfter);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Chainlink read with the full set of sanity checks: positive answer, complete round,\n    ///      non-future timestamp, and a bounded age.\n    function _readFeed(IAggregatorV3 feed, uint256 scale, uint256 maxAge) internal view returns (uint256) {\n        (uint80 roundId, int256 answer, uint256 startedAt, uint256 updatedAt, uint80 answeredInRound) =\n            feed.latestRoundData();\n        if (answer <= 0) revert PriceNotPositive();\n        if (updatedAt == 0 || startedAt == 0 || answeredInRound < roundId) revert PriceRoundIncomplete();\n        if (updatedAt > block.timestamp) revert PriceRoundIncomplete();\n        if (block.timestamp - updatedAt > maxAge) revert PriceStale(updatedAt, maxAge);\n        return uint256(answer) * scale;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice USD value (18 decimals) of a collateral amount.\n    function _collateralValueUsd(uint256 amount) internal view returns (uint256) {\n        if (amount == 0) return 0;\n        return Math.mulDiv(amount, collateralPrice(), collateralUnit);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Collateral amount worth `valueUsd`, rounded down.\n    function _collateralForValueUsd(uint256 valueUsd) internal view returns (uint256) {\n        return Math.mulDiv(valueUsd, collateralUnit, collateralPrice());\n    }\n\n    /// @notice USD value (18 decimals) of a debt amount, with an explicit rounding direction.\n    function _debtValueUsd(uint256 amount, Math.Rounding rounding) internal view returns (uint256) {\n        if (amount == 0) return 0;\n        return Math.mulDiv(amount, debtPrice(), debtUnit, rounding);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Debt amount worth `valueUsd`, rounded down.\n    function _debtForValueUsd(uint256 valueUsd) internal view returns (uint256) {\n        return Math.mulDiv(valueUsd, debtUnit, debtPrice());\n    }\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                                  VIEWS                                 */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @notice Current debt of `account` in debt-token units, interest included.\n    function debtOf(address account) public view returns (uint256) {\n        return _debtOf(account, currentBorrowIndex());\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Rounds the borrower's debt up.\n    function _debtOf(address account, uint256 index) internal view returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 scaled = scaledDebtOf[account];\n        if (scaled == 0) return 0;\n        return Math.mulDiv(scaled, index, RAY, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Total outstanding debt across all positions, interest included.\n    function totalDebt() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return Math.mulDiv(totalScaledDebt, currentBorrowIndex(), RAY, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Debt tokens sitting idle in the pool and available to borrow.\n    function availableLiquidity() public view returns (uint256) {\n        return debtToken.balanceOf(address(this));\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Position snapshot in 18-decimal USD terms.\n    /// @return collateral Collateral locked, in collateral-token units.\n    /// @return debt Debt owed, in debt-token units.\n    /// @return collateralValue USD value of the collateral.\n    /// @return debtValue USD value of the debt.\n    /// @return maxDebtValue USD debt the collateral supports at 70% LTV.\n    /// @return liquidationValue USD debt value at which liquidation unlocks (85%).\n    function positionOf(address account)\n        external\n        view\n        returns (\n            uint256 collateral,\n            uint256 debt,\n            uint256 collateralValue,\n            uint256 debtValue,\n            uint256 maxDebtValue,\n            uint256 liquidationValue\n        )\n    {\n        collateral = collateralOf[account];\n        debt = debtOf(account);\n        collateralValue = _collateralValueUsd(collateral);\n        debtValue = _debtValueUsd(debt, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n        maxDebtValue = Math.mulDiv(collateralValue, MAX_LTV_BPS, BPS);\n        liquidationValue = Math.mulDiv(collateralValue, LIQUIDATION_THRESHOLD_BPS, BPS);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Health factor, 18 decimals. Below 1e18 the position is liquidatable.\n    ///         `type(uint256).max` when there is no debt.\n    function healthFactor(address account) external view returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 debtValue = _debtValueUsd(debtOf(account), Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n        if (debtValue == 0) return type(uint256).max;\n        uint256 liquidationValue = Math.mulDiv(_collateralValueUsd(collateralOf[account]), LIQUIDATION_THRESHOLD_BPS, BPS);\n        return Math.mulDiv(liquidationValue, USD_UNIT, debtValue);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice True when `account` can currently be liquidated.\n    function isLiquidatable(address account) external view returns (bool) {\n        uint256 debtValue = _debtValueUsd(debtOf(account), Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n        if (debtValue == 0) return false;\n        uint256 liquidationValue = Math.mulDiv(_collateralValueUsd(collateralOf[account]), LIQUIDATION_THRESHOLD_BPS, BPS);\n        return debtValue > liquidationValue;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Additional debt `account` could take on right now.\n    function maxBorrowable(address account) external view returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 maxDebtValue = Math.mulDiv(_collateralValueUsd(collateralOf[account]), MAX_LTV_BPS, BPS);\n        uint256 debtValue = _debtValueUsd(debtOf(account), Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n        if (debtValue >= maxDebtValue) return 0;\n        uint256 headroom = _debtForValueUsd(maxDebtValue - debtValue);\n        uint256 liquidity = availableLiquidity();\n        return headroom > liquidity ? liquidity : headroom;\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Reverts unless `account`'s debt is within 70% of `collateral`'s value.\n    ///      Uses the already-accrued index, so callers must `accrue()` first.\n    function _requireWithinBorrowPower(address account, uint256 collateral) internal view {\n        uint256 debt = _debtOf(account, borrowIndex);\n        if (debt == 0) return;\n        uint256 debtValue = _debtValueUsd(debt, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n        uint256 maxDebtValue = Math.mulDiv(_collateralValueUsd(collateral), MAX_LTV_BPS, BPS);\n        if (debtValue > maxDebtValue) revert ExceedsBorrowPower(debtValue, maxDebtValue);\n    }\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                             OPERATOR ACTIONS                           */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @notice Supply borrowable liquidity. Open to anyone — funds become pool reserves and are\n    ///         only recoverable by the owner, so this is an operator/treasury action.\n    function fund(uint256 amount) external nonReentrant {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        debtToken.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n        emit ReserveFunded(msg.sender, amount);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Withdraw idle liquidity (principal + accrued interest that has been repaid).\n    /// @dev Bounded by the pool's actual balance; outstanding debt is untouched by design.\n    function withdrawReserves(uint256 amount, address to) external onlyOwner nonReentrant {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (to == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        uint256 available = availableLiquidity();\n        if (amount > available) revert InsufficientLiquidity(amount, available);\n        debtToken.safeTransfer(to, amount);\n        emit ReserveWithdrawn(to, amount);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Update the flat annual borrow rate. Existing interest is accrued at the old rate first.\n    function setBorrowRate(uint256 newRateBps) external onlyOwner {\n        if (newRateBps > MAX_BORROW_RATE_BPS) revert InvalidParameter();\n        accrue();\n        uint256 old = borrowRateBps;\n        borrowRateBps = newRateBps;\n        emit BorrowRateSet(old, newRateBps);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Update the dust floor for positions.\n    function setMinDebt(uint256 newMinDebt) external onlyOwner {\n        if (newMinDebt == 0 || newMinDebt > MAX_MIN_DEBT * debtUnit) revert InvalidParameter();\n        uint256 old = minDebt;\n        minDebt = newMinDebt;\n        emit MinDebtSet(old, newMinDebt);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Update oracle staleness windows, e.g. after a Chainlink heartbeat change.\n    function setStalenessWindows(uint256 collateralWindow, uint256 debtWindow) external onlyOwner {\n        _validateStaleness(collateralWindow, debtWindow);\n        collateralFeedStaleAfter = collateralWindow;\n        debtFeedStaleAfter = debtWindow;\n        emit StalenessWindowsSet(collateralWindow, debtWindow);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Halt deposits, borrows, withdrawals and liquidations. Repayment stays open.\n    /// @dev A single key that can freeze the market is a real censorship/liveness risk —\n    ///      see NOTES.md. Owner must be a multisig, ideally with a timelock on non-emergency ops.\n    function pause() external onlyOwner {\n        _pause();\n    }\n\n    function unpause() external onlyOwner {\n        _unpause();\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Recover tokens accidentally sent here. Cannot touch the collateral or debt asset.\n    function sweep(address token, address to, uint256 amount) external onlyOwner nonReentrant {\n        if (token == address(collateralToken) || token == address(debtToken)) revert InvalidParameter();\n        if (to == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        IERC20(token).safeTransfer(to, amount);\n    }\n\n    function _validateStaleness(uint256 collateralWindow, uint256 debtWindow) internal pure {\n        if (\n            collateralWindow < MIN_STALENESS_WINDOW || collateralWindow > MAX_STALENESS_WINDOW\n                || debtWindow < MIN_STALENESS_WINDOW || debtWindow > MAX_STALENESS_WINDOW\n        ) revert InvalidParameter();\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":5507,"cache_read_input_tokens":42024,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":5507},"output_tokens":62,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e022dfc2-ee4b-49d0-904f-ee7e007080f4","uuid":"bf45ac05-14f4-4c61-ac82-1a377cc66d81","timestamp":"2026-07-28T12:33:46.211Z","request_id":"req_011CdUTdmSGJFJa8nvBt2cK4"}
{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01BKKhAV9PUwV5ShyHfgJh5z","type":"tool_result","content":"File created successfully at: /Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/security-goal-002/2026-07-28T122814Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/src/LendingPool.sol (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e022dfc2-ee4b-49d0-904f-ee7e007080f4","uuid":"9deba5fe-45de-4dfb-9071-dc4065e4d554","timestamp":"2026-07-28T12:33:46.246Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"create","filePath":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/security-goal-002/2026-07-28T122814Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/src/LendingPool.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity 0.8.28;\n\nimport {IERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol\";\nimport {IERC20Metadata} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/extensions/IERC20Metadata.sol\";\nimport {SafeERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol\";\nimport {ReentrancyGuard} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/ReentrancyGuard.sol\";\nimport {Pausable} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/Pausable.sol\";\nimport {Ownable, Ownable2Step} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable2Step.sol\";\nimport {Math} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/math/Math.sol\";\n\nimport {IAggregatorV3} from \"./interfaces/IAggregatorV3.sol\";\n\n/// @title LendingPool\n/// @notice Single-pair borrowing market: lock `collateralToken` (WETH), borrow `debtToken` (USDC)\n///         against it up to 70% LTV. Debt accrues a flat annual rate. Positions above the 85%\n///         liquidation threshold can be partially closed by anyone for a 5% collateral bonus.\n/// @dev    Lending liquidity is supplied by the operator (`fund`), not by public depositors —\n///         there are no supply-side shares. See NOTES.md for the full trust model.\ncontract LendingPool is Ownable2Step, Pausable, ReentrancyGuard {\n    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                                CONSTANTS                               */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    uint256 public constant BPS = 10_000;\n    /// @dev Interest index precision (27 decimals), Aave-style.\n    uint256 public constant RAY = 1e27;\n    uint256 public constant SECONDS_PER_YEAR = 365 days;\n    /// @dev All USD values are carried internally with 18 decimals.\n    uint256 internal constant USD_UNIT = 1e18;\n\n    /// @notice Max borrow power: debt value may not exceed 70% of collateral value.\n    uint256 public constant MAX_LTV_BPS = 7_000;\n    /// @notice A position is liquidatable once debt value exceeds 85% of collateral value.\n    uint256 public constant LIQUIDATION_THRESHOLD_BPS = 8_500;\n    /// @notice Liquidator seizes collateral worth repaid debt + 5%.\n    uint256 public constant LIQUIDATION_BONUS_BPS = 500;\n    /// @notice At most 50% of a position's debt can be repaid in a single liquidation.\n    uint256 public constant MAX_CLOSE_FACTOR_BPS = 5_000;\n\n    /// @notice Hard ceiling on the owner-settable borrow rate (50% APR).\n    uint256 public constant MAX_BORROW_RATE_BPS = 5_000;\n    /// @notice Bounds on the owner-settable oracle staleness windows.\n    uint256 public constant MIN_STALENESS_WINDOW = 10 minutes;\n    uint256 public constant MAX_STALENESS_WINDOW = 2 days;\n    /// @notice Bound on the owner-settable dust floor (in debt-token units).\n    uint256 public constant MAX_MIN_DEBT = 100_000;\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                               IMMUTABLES                               */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @notice Collateral asset (WETH on mainnet).\n    IERC20 public immutable collateralToken;\n    /// @notice Borrowable asset (USDC on mainnet).\n    IERC20 public immutable debtToken;\n    /// @dev 10 ** decimals() of each token, read at construction. Never hardcode 1e18.\n    uint256 public immutable collateralUnit;\n    uint256 public immutable debtUnit;\n\n    /// @notice Chainlink feed for collateral, quoted in USD (ETH/USD).\n    IAggregatorV3 public immutable collateralFeed;\n    /// @notice Chainlink feed for the borrowed asset, quoted in USD (USDC/USD).\n    IAggregatorV3 public immutable debtFeed;\n    /// @dev Multiplier that lifts each feed's answer to 18 decimals.\n    uint256 internal immutable collateralFeedScale;\n    uint256 internal immutable debtFeedScale;\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                                 STORAGE                                */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @notice Flat annual borrow rate in basis points.\n    uint256 public borrowRateBps;\n    /// @notice Cumulative interest index, RAY-scaled. Monotonically non-decreasing.\n    uint256 public borrowIndex;\n    /// @notice Timestamp the index was last brought up to date.\n    uint256 public lastAccrualTime;\n    /// @notice Sum of all index-scaled debt.\n    uint256 public totalScaledDebt;\n\n    /// @notice Minimum position debt, in debt-token units. Keeps positions worth liquidating.\n    uint256 public minDebt;\n    /// @notice Max age of a collateral-feed answer before it is rejected.\n    uint256 public collateralFeedStaleAfter;\n    /// @notice Max age of a debt-feed answer before it is rejected.\n    uint256 public debtFeedStaleAfter;\n\n    /// @notice Collateral balance per borrower, in collateral-token units.\n    mapping(address account => uint256 amount) public collateralOf;\n    /// @notice Index-scaled debt per borrower. Actual debt = scaled * borrowIndex / RAY.\n    mapping(address account => uint256 scaled) public scaledDebtOf;\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                                  EVENTS                                */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    event CollateralDeposited(address indexed account, address indexed payer, uint256 amount);\n    event CollateralWithdrawn(address indexed account, address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n    event Borrowed(address indexed account, address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n    event Repaid(address indexed account, address indexed payer, uint256 amount);\n    event Liquidated(\n        address indexed borrower,\n        address indexed liquidator,\n        uint256 debtRepaid,\n        uint256 collateralSeized,\n        uint256 remainingDebt\n    );\n    /// @dev Emitted when a liquidation exhausts the borrower's collateral and leaves debt behind.\n    event BadDebt(address indexed borrower, uint256 remainingDebt);\n    event Accrued(uint256 borrowIndex, uint256 timestamp);\n    event BorrowRateSet(uint256 oldRateBps, uint256 newRateBps);\n    event MinDebtSet(uint256 oldMinDebt, uint256 newMinDebt);\n    event StalenessWindowsSet(uint256 collateralFeedStaleAfter, uint256 debtFeedStaleAfter);\n    event ReserveFunded(address indexed from, uint256 amount);\n    event ReserveWithdrawn(address indexed to, uint256 amount);\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                                  ERRORS                                */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    error ZeroAddress();\n    error ZeroAmount();\n    error InvalidDecimals();\n    error InvalidParameter();\n    error PriceNotPositive();\n    error PriceStale(uint256 updatedAt, uint256 maxAge);\n    error PriceRoundIncomplete();\n    error InsufficientCollateral();\n    error ExceedsBorrowPower(uint256 debtValue, uint256 maxDebtValue);\n    error InsufficientLiquidity(uint256 requested, uint256 available);\n    error NoDebt();\n    error DebtBelowMinimum(uint256 debt, uint256 minDebt);\n    error PositionHealthy(uint256 debtValue, uint256 liquidationValue);\n    error NothingToLiquidate();\n    error SlippageExceeded(uint256 seized, uint256 minSeized);\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                              CONSTRUCTION                              */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @param owner_ Operator address. Must be a multisig/timelock on mainnet.\n    /// @param collateralToken_ WETH.\n    /// @param debtToken_ USDC.\n    /// @param collateralFeed_ Chainlink ETH/USD.\n    /// @param debtFeed_ Chainlink USDC/USD.\n    /// @param borrowRateBps_ Flat annual rate, in bps.\n    /// @param minDebt_ Dust floor in debt-token units (e.g. 100e6 = 100 USDC).\n    /// @param collateralFeedStaleAfter_ Staleness window for the collateral feed.\n    /// @param debtFeedStaleAfter_ Staleness window for the debt feed.\n    constructor(\n        address owner_,\n        address collateralToken_,\n        address debtToken_,\n        address collateralFeed_,\n        address debtFeed_,\n        uint256 borrowRateBps_,\n        uint256 minDebt_,\n        uint256 collateralFeedStaleAfter_,\n        uint256 debtFeedStaleAfter_\n    ) Ownable(owner_) {\n        if (\n            owner_ == address(0) || collateralToken_ == address(0) || debtToken_ == address(0)\n                || collateralFeed_ == address(0) || debtFeed_ == address(0)\n        ) revert ZeroAddress();\n        if (collateralToken_ == debtToken_) revert InvalidParameter();\n\n        collateralToken = IERC20(collateralToken_);\n        debtToken = IERC20(debtToken_);\n\n        // Decimals are read from the tokens, never assumed. USDC is 6, WETH is 18.\n        uint8 colDec = IERC20Metadata(collateralToken_).decimals();\n        uint8 debtDec = IERC20Metadata(debtToken_).decimals();\n        if (colDec > 18 || debtDec > 18) revert InvalidDecimals();\n        collateralUnit = 10 ** colDec;\n        debtUnit = 10 ** debtDec;\n\n        collateralFeed = IAggregatorV3(collateralFeed_);\n        debtFeed = IAggregatorV3(debtFeed_);\n        uint8 colFeedDec = IAggregatorV3(collateralFeed_).decimals();\n        uint8 debtFeedDec = IAggregatorV3(debtFeed_).decimals();\n        if (colFeedDec > 18 || debtFeedDec > 18) revert InvalidDecimals();\n        collateralFeedScale = 10 ** (18 - colFeedDec);\n        debtFeedScale = 10 ** (18 - debtFeedDec);\n\n        if (borrowRateBps_ > MAX_BORROW_RATE_BPS) revert InvalidParameter();\n        if (minDebt_ == 0 || minDebt_ > MAX_MIN_DEBT * debtUnit) revert InvalidParameter();\n        _validateStaleness(collateralFeedStaleAfter_, debtFeedStaleAfter_);\n\n        borrowRateBps = borrowRateBps_;\n        minDebt = minDebt_;\n        collateralFeedStaleAfter = collateralFeedStaleAfter_;\n        debtFeedStaleAfter = debtFeedStaleAfter_;\n\n        borrowIndex = RAY;\n        lastAccrualTime = block.timestamp;\n\n        emit BorrowRateSet(0, borrowRateBps_);\n        emit MinDebtSet(0, minDebt_);\n        emit StalenessWindowsSet(collateralFeedStaleAfter_, debtFeedStaleAfter_);\n    }\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                           INTEREST ACCRUAL                             */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @notice Brings the borrow index up to the current block. Callable by anyone.\n    /// @dev Every state-changing entry point calls this first, so the index is never stale\n    ///      when health is evaluated.\n    function accrue() public {\n        uint256 elapsed = block.timestamp - lastAccrualTime;\n        if (elapsed == 0) return;\n        lastAccrualTime = block.timestamp;\n\n        uint256 rate = borrowRateBps;\n        // No debt outstanding, or a zero rate: nothing to compound, but the clock still moves\n        // forward so idle time can never be charged retroactively.\n        if (rate == 0 || totalScaledDebt == 0) return;\n\n        uint256 newIndex = _indexAt(borrowIndex, rate, elapsed);\n        borrowIndex = newIndex;\n        emit Accrued(newIndex, block.timestamp);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Simple interest applied over `elapsed`, compounded across accruals.\n    function _indexAt(uint256 index, uint256 rate, uint256 elapsed) internal pure returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 growth = RAY + Math.mulDiv(RAY * rate, elapsed, BPS * SECONDS_PER_YEAR);\n        return Math.mulDiv(index, growth, RAY);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Borrow index as it would be after accruing to the current block.\n    function currentBorrowIndex() public view returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 elapsed = block.timestamp - lastAccrualTime;\n        uint256 rate = borrowRateBps;\n        if (elapsed == 0 || rate == 0 || totalScaledDebt == 0) return borrowIndex;\n        return _indexAt(borrowIndex, rate, elapsed);\n    }\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                            BORROWER ACTIONS                            */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @notice Lock collateral for `onBehalfOf`. Tokens are pulled from `msg.sender`.\n    /// @dev Credits the balance actually received, so a fee-on-transfer collateral token\n    ///      could never be credited for more than the pool holds.\n    function depositCollateral(uint256 amount, address onBehalfOf) external nonReentrant whenNotPaused {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (onBehalfOf == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        accrue();\n\n        uint256 balanceBefore = collateralToken.balanceOf(address(this));\n        collateralToken.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n        uint256 received = collateralToken.balanceOf(address(this)) - balanceBefore;\n        if (received == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n        collateralOf[onBehalfOf] += received;\n        emit CollateralDeposited(onBehalfOf, msg.sender, received);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Withdraw collateral. The position must stay within the 70% LTV afterwards.\n    function withdrawCollateral(uint256 amount, address to) external nonReentrant whenNotPaused {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (to == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        accrue();\n\n        uint256 balance = collateralOf[msg.sender];\n        if (amount > balance) revert InsufficientCollateral();\n\n        // Effects before the external transfer (CEI).\n        uint256 remaining = balance - amount;\n        collateralOf[msg.sender] = remaining;\n        _requireWithinBorrowPower(msg.sender, remaining);\n\n        collateralToken.safeTransfer(to, amount);\n        emit CollateralWithdrawn(msg.sender, to, amount);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Borrow `amount` of the debt token against already-locked collateral.\n    function borrow(uint256 amount, address to) external nonReentrant whenNotPaused {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (to == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        accrue();\n\n        uint256 available = debtToken.balanceOf(address(this));\n        if (amount > available) revert InsufficientLiquidity(amount, available);\n\n        // Round the borrower's scaled debt up: rounding always favours the pool.\n        uint256 scaledDelta = Math.mulDiv(amount, RAY, borrowIndex, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n        scaledDebtOf[msg.sender] += scaledDelta;\n        totalScaledDebt += scaledDelta;\n\n        uint256 newDebt = _debtOf(msg.sender, borrowIndex);\n        if (newDebt < minDebt) revert DebtBelowMinimum(newDebt, minDebt);\n        _requireWithinBorrowPower(msg.sender, collateralOf[msg.sender]);\n\n        debtToken.safeTransfer(to, amount);\n        emit Borrowed(msg.sender, to, amount);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Repay debt for `onBehalfOf`. Pass `type(uint256).max` to clear the position.\n    /// @dev Intentionally callable while paused so borrowers can always de-risk.\n    /// @return repaid The debt-token amount actually pulled from `msg.sender`.\n    function repay(uint256 amount, address onBehalfOf) external nonReentrant returns (uint256 repaid) {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (onBehalfOf == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        accrue();\n\n        uint256 index = borrowIndex;\n        uint256 debt = _debtOf(onBehalfOf, index);\n        if (debt == 0) revert NoDebt();\n\n        repaid = amount > debt ? debt : amount;\n\n        uint256 scaledDelta;\n        if (repaid == debt) {\n            // Full repayment: burn the exact scaled balance so no dust can survive rounding.\n            scaledDelta = scaledDebtOf[onBehalfOf];\n        } else {\n            // Round the debt reduction down: rounding always favours the pool.\n            scaledDelta = Math.mulDiv(repaid, RAY, index, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        }\n\n        uint256 remainingScaled = scaledDebtOf[onBehalfOf] - scaledDelta;\n        scaledDebtOf[onBehalfOf] = remainingScaled;\n        totalScaledDebt -= scaledDelta;\n\n        uint256 remainingDebt = _debtOf(onBehalfOf, index);\n        if (remainingDebt != 0 && remainingDebt < minDebt) revert DebtBelowMinimum(remainingDebt, minDebt);\n\n        debtToken.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), repaid);\n        emit Repaid(onBehalfOf, msg.sender, repaid);\n    }\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                              LIQUIDATION                               */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @notice Repay part of an unhealthy position's debt and seize the matching collateral\n    ///         plus a 5% bonus. Callable by anyone.\n    /// @param borrower Position to liquidate.\n    /// @param repayAmount Debt tokens the caller is willing to repay; capped at the close factor.\n    /// @param minCollateralOut Slippage guard — revert if less collateral than this is seized.\n    /// @return repaid Debt tokens actually pulled from the caller.\n    /// @return seized Collateral tokens sent to the caller.\n    function liquidate(address borrower, uint256 repayAmount, uint256 minCollateralOut)\n        external\n        nonReentrant\n        whenNotPaused\n        returns (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized)\n    {\n        if (borrower == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        if (repayAmount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        accrue();\n\n        uint256 index = borrowIndex;\n        uint256 debt = _debtOf(borrower, index);\n        if (debt == 0) revert NoDebt();\n\n        uint256 collateral = collateralOf[borrower];\n        uint256 collateralValue = _collateralValueUsd(collateral);\n        uint256 debtValue = _debtValueUsd(debt, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n        uint256 liquidationValue = Math.mulDiv(collateralValue, LIQUIDATION_THRESHOLD_BPS, BPS);\n        if (debtValue <= liquidationValue) revert PositionHealthy(debtValue, liquidationValue);\n\n        bool seizedAll;\n        (repaid, seized, seizedAll) = _computeLiquidation(debt, collateral, repayAmount);\n        if (repaid == 0 || seized == 0) revert NothingToLiquidate();\n        if (seized < minCollateralOut) revert SlippageExceeded(seized, minCollateralOut);\n\n        uint256 scaledDelta;\n        if (repaid == debt) {\n            scaledDelta = scaledDebtOf[borrower];\n        } else {\n            scaledDelta = Math.mulDiv(repaid, RAY, index, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        }\n\n        // Effects.\n        scaledDebtOf[borrower] -= scaledDelta;\n        totalScaledDebt -= scaledDelta;\n        collateralOf[borrower] = collateral - seized;\n\n        uint256 remainingDebt = _debtOf(borrower, index);\n        // A position left with dust debt is not worth liquidating later; only allow it when the\n        // collateral is exhausted (nothing more a liquidator could take anyway).\n        if (!seizedAll && remainingDebt != 0 && remainingDebt < minDebt) {\n            revert DebtBelowMinimum(remainingDebt, minDebt);\n        }\n\n        // Interactions.\n        debtToken.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), repaid);\n        collateralToken.safeTransfer(msg.sender, seized);\n\n        emit Liquidated(borrower, msg.sender, repaid, seized, remainingDebt);\n        if (seizedAll && remainingDebt != 0) emit BadDebt(borrower, remainingDebt);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Off-chain helper: what a liquidation of `borrower` for `repayAmount` would do.\n    /// @dev Reverts with the same errors as `liquidate` for a healthy or debt-free position.\n    function previewLiquidation(address borrower, uint256 repayAmount)\n        external\n        view\n        returns (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized)\n    {\n        uint256 index = currentBorrowIndex();\n        uint256 debt = _debtOf(borrower, index);\n        if (debt == 0) revert NoDebt();\n\n        uint256 collateral = collateralOf[borrower];\n        uint256 debtValue = _debtValueUsd(debt, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n        uint256 liquidationValue = Math.mulDiv(_collateralValueUsd(collateral), LIQUIDATION_THRESHOLD_BPS, BPS);\n        if (debtValue <= liquidationValue) revert PositionHealthy(debtValue, liquidationValue);\n\n        (repaid, seized,) = _computeLiquidation(debt, collateral, repayAmount);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Splits liquidation sizing out of `liquidate` so the preview cannot drift from it.\n    function _computeLiquidation(uint256 debt, uint256 collateral, uint256 requestedRepay)\n        internal\n        view\n        returns (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized, bool seizedAll)\n    {\n        uint256 maxRepay = Math.mulDiv(debt, MAX_CLOSE_FACTOR_BPS, BPS);\n        // Never leave a sub-minimum remainder that no one would bother liquidating.\n        if (debt - maxRepay < minDebt) maxRepay = debt;\n\n        repaid = requestedRepay > maxRepay ? maxRepay : requestedRepay;\n        if (repaid == 0) return (0, 0, false);\n\n        seized = _seizeForRepay(repaid);\n        if (seized >= collateral) {\n            // Underwater position: hand over what is left and shrink the repayment to match,\n            // so the liquidator still earns the bonus on what they actually get.\n            seized = collateral;\n            seizedAll = true;\n            repaid = _repayForSeize(collateral);\n            if (repaid > debt) repaid = debt;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Collateral owed to a liquidator for repaying `repayAmount`, including the bonus.\n    ///      Rounds down: the pool never over-pays a liquidator on rounding.\n    function _seizeForRepay(uint256 repayAmount) internal view returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 valueUsd = _debtValueUsd(repayAmount, Math.Rounding.Floor);\n        uint256 withBonus = Math.mulDiv(valueUsd, BPS + LIQUIDATION_BONUS_BPS, BPS);\n        return _collateralForValueUsd(withBonus);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Inverse of `_seizeForRepay`: debt covered by seizing exactly `collateralAmount`.\n    function _repayForSeize(uint256 collateralAmount) internal view returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 valueUsd = _collateralValueUsd(collateralAmount);\n        uint256 withoutBonus = Math.mulDiv(valueUsd, BPS, BPS + LIQUIDATION_BONUS_BPS);\n        return _debtForValueUsd(withoutBonus);\n    }\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                                 PRICING                                */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @notice Collateral price in USD, 18 decimals.\n    function collateralPrice() public view returns (uint256) {\n        return _readFeed(collateralFeed, collateralFeedScale, collateralFeedStaleAfter);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Debt-token price in USD, 18 decimals. Not assumed to be exactly $1.\n    function debtPrice() public view returns (uint256) {\n        return _readFeed(debtFeed, debtFeedScale, debtFeedStaleAfter);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Chainlink read with the full set of sanity checks: positive answer, complete round,\n    ///      non-future timestamp, and a bounded age.\n    function _readFeed(IAggregatorV3 feed, uint256 scale, uint256 maxAge) internal view returns (uint256) {\n        (uint80 roundId, int256 answer, uint256 startedAt, uint256 updatedAt, uint80 answeredInRound) =\n            feed.latestRoundData();\n        if (answer <= 0) revert PriceNotPositive();\n        if (updatedAt == 0 || startedAt == 0 || answeredInRound < roundId) revert PriceRoundIncomplete();\n        if (updatedAt > block.timestamp) revert PriceRoundIncomplete();\n        if (block.timestamp - updatedAt > maxAge) revert PriceStale(updatedAt, maxAge);\n        return uint256(answer) * scale;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice USD value (18 decimals) of a collateral amount.\n    function _collateralValueUsd(uint256 amount) internal view returns (uint256) {\n        if (amount == 0) return 0;\n        return Math.mulDiv(amount, collateralPrice(), collateralUnit);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Collateral amount worth `valueUsd`, rounded down.\n    function _collateralForValueUsd(uint256 valueUsd) internal view returns (uint256) {\n        return Math.mulDiv(valueUsd, collateralUnit, collateralPrice());\n    }\n\n    /// @notice USD value (18 decimals) of a debt amount, with an explicit rounding direction.\n    function _debtValueUsd(uint256 amount, Math.Rounding rounding) internal view returns (uint256) {\n        if (amount == 0) return 0;\n        return Math.mulDiv(amount, debtPrice(), debtUnit, rounding);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Debt amount worth `valueUsd`, rounded down.\n    function _debtForValueUsd(uint256 valueUsd) internal view returns (uint256) {\n        return Math.mulDiv(valueUsd, debtUnit, debtPrice());\n    }\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                                  VIEWS                                 */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @notice Current debt of `account` in debt-token units, interest included.\n    function debtOf(address account) public view returns (uint256) {\n        return _debtOf(account, currentBorrowIndex());\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Rounds the borrower's debt up.\n    function _debtOf(address account, uint256 index) internal view returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 scaled = scaledDebtOf[account];\n        if (scaled == 0) return 0;\n        return Math.mulDiv(scaled, index, RAY, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Total outstanding debt across all positions, interest included.\n    function totalDebt() external view returns (uint256) {\n        return Math.mulDiv(totalScaledDebt, currentBorrowIndex(), RAY, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Debt tokens sitting idle in the pool and available to borrow.\n    function availableLiquidity() public view returns (uint256) {\n        return debtToken.balanceOf(address(this));\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Position snapshot in 18-decimal USD terms.\n    /// @return collateral Collateral locked, in collateral-token units.\n    /// @return debt Debt owed, in debt-token units.\n    /// @return collateralValue USD value of the collateral.\n    /// @return debtValue USD value of the debt.\n    /// @return maxDebtValue USD debt the collateral supports at 70% LTV.\n    /// @return liquidationValue USD debt value at which liquidation unlocks (85%).\n    function positionOf(address account)\n        external\n        view\n        returns (\n            uint256 collateral,\n            uint256 debt,\n            uint256 collateralValue,\n            uint256 debtValue,\n            uint256 maxDebtValue,\n            uint256 liquidationValue\n        )\n    {\n        collateral = collateralOf[account];\n        debt = debtOf(account);\n        collateralValue = _collateralValueUsd(collateral);\n        debtValue = _debtValueUsd(debt, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n        maxDebtValue = Math.mulDiv(collateralValue, MAX_LTV_BPS, BPS);\n        liquidationValue = Math.mulDiv(collateralValue, LIQUIDATION_THRESHOLD_BPS, BPS);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Health factor, 18 decimals. Below 1e18 the position is liquidatable.\n    ///         `type(uint256).max` when there is no debt.\n    function healthFactor(address account) external view returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 debtValue = _debtValueUsd(debtOf(account), Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n        if (debtValue == 0) return type(uint256).max;\n        uint256 liquidationValue = Math.mulDiv(_collateralValueUsd(collateralOf[account]), LIQUIDATION_THRESHOLD_BPS, BPS);\n        return Math.mulDiv(liquidationValue, USD_UNIT, debtValue);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice True when `account` can currently be liquidated.\n    function isLiquidatable(address account) external view returns (bool) {\n        uint256 debtValue = _debtValueUsd(debtOf(account), Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n        if (debtValue == 0) return false;\n        uint256 liquidationValue = Math.mulDiv(_collateralValueUsd(collateralOf[account]), LIQUIDATION_THRESHOLD_BPS, BPS);\n        return debtValue > liquidationValue;\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Additional debt `account` could take on right now.\n    function maxBorrowable(address account) external view returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 maxDebtValue = Math.mulDiv(_collateralValueUsd(collateralOf[account]), MAX_LTV_BPS, BPS);\n        uint256 debtValue = _debtValueUsd(debtOf(account), Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n        if (debtValue >= maxDebtValue) return 0;\n        uint256 headroom = _debtForValueUsd(maxDebtValue - debtValue);\n        uint256 liquidity = availableLiquidity();\n        return headroom > liquidity ? liquidity : headroom;\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Reverts unless `account`'s debt is within 70% of `collateral`'s value.\n    ///      Uses the already-accrued index, so callers must `accrue()` first.\n    function _requireWithinBorrowPower(address account, uint256 collateral) internal view {\n        uint256 debt = _debtOf(account, borrowIndex);\n        if (debt == 0) return;\n        uint256 debtValue = _debtValueUsd(debt, Math.Rounding.Ceil);\n        uint256 maxDebtValue = Math.mulDiv(_collateralValueUsd(collateral), MAX_LTV_BPS, BPS);\n        if (debtValue > maxDebtValue) revert ExceedsBorrowPower(debtValue, maxDebtValue);\n    }\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                             OPERATOR ACTIONS                           */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    /// @notice Supply borrowable liquidity. Open to anyone — funds become pool reserves and are\n    ///         only recoverable by the owner, so this is an operator/treasury action.\n    function fund(uint256 amount) external nonReentrant {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        debtToken.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n        emit ReserveFunded(msg.sender, amount);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Withdraw idle liquidity (principal + accrued interest that has been repaid).\n    /// @dev Bounded by the pool's actual balance; outstanding debt is untouched by design.\n    function withdrawReserves(uint256 amount, address to) external onlyOwner nonReentrant {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n        if (to == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        uint256 available = availableLiquidity();\n        if (amount > available) revert InsufficientLiquidity(amount, available);\n        debtToken.safeTransfer(to, amount);\n        emit ReserveWithdrawn(to, amount);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Update the flat annual borrow rate. Existing interest is accrued at the old rate first.\n    function setBorrowRate(uint256 newRateBps) external onlyOwner {\n        if (newRateBps > MAX_BORROW_RATE_BPS) revert InvalidParameter();\n        accrue();\n        uint256 old = borrowRateBps;\n        borrowRateBps = newRateBps;\n        emit BorrowRateSet(old, newRateBps);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Update the dust floor for positions.\n    function setMinDebt(uint256 newMinDebt) external onlyOwner {\n        if (newMinDebt == 0 || newMinDebt > MAX_MIN_DEBT * debtUnit) revert InvalidParameter();\n        uint256 old = minDebt;\n        minDebt = newMinDebt;\n        emit MinDebtSet(old, newMinDebt);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Update oracle staleness windows, e.g. after a Chainlink heartbeat change.\n    function setStalenessWindows(uint256 collateralWindow, uint256 debtWindow) external onlyOwner {\n        _validateStaleness(collateralWindow, debtWindow);\n        collateralFeedStaleAfter = collateralWindow;\n        debtFeedStaleAfter = debtWindow;\n        emit StalenessWindowsSet(collateralWindow, debtWindow);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Halt deposits, borrows, withdrawals and liquidations. Repayment stays open.\n    /// @dev A single key that can freeze the market is a real censorship/liveness risk —\n    ///      see NOTES.md. Owner must be a multisig, ideally with a timelock on non-emergency ops.\n    function pause() external onlyOwner {\n        _pause();\n    }\n\n    function unpause() external onlyOwner {\n        _unpause();\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Recover tokens accidentally sent here. Cannot touch the collateral or debt asset.\n    function sweep(address token, address to, uint256 amount) external onlyOwner nonReentrant {\n        if (token == address(collateralToken) || token == address(debtToken)) revert InvalidParameter();\n        if (to == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        IERC20(token).safeTransfer(to, amount);\n    }\n\n    function _validateStaleness(uint256 collateralWindow, uint256 debtWindow) internal pure {\n        if (\n            collateralWindow < MIN_STALENESS_WINDOW || collateralWindow > MAX_STALENESS_WINDOW\n                || debtWindow < MIN_STALENESS_WINDOW || debtWindow > MAX_STALENESS_WINDOW\n        ) revert InvalidParameter();\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}
