SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity 0.8.28;\n\nimport {Test} from \"forge-std/Test.sol\";\nimport {IERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol\";\nimport {Ownable} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol\";\nimport {Pausable} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/Pausable.sol\";\nimport {ReentrancyGuard} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/ReentrancyGuard.sol\";\n\nimport {LendingPool} from \"../src/LendingPool.sol\";\nimport {MockERC20} from \"./mocks/MockERC20.sol\";\nimport {MockAggregator} from \"./mocks/MockAggregator.sol\";\nimport {ReentrantToken} from \"./mocks/ReentrantToken.sol\";\n\ncontract LendingPoolTest is Test {\n    LendingPool internal pool;\n    MockERC20 internal weth;\n    MockERC20 internal usdc;\n    MockAggregator internal ethFeed;\n    MockAggregator internal usdcFeed;\n\n    address internal owner = address(0xA11CE);\n    address internal alice = address(0xB0B);\n    address internal keeper = address(0xC0FFEE);\n\n    uint256 internal constant RATE_BPS = 500; // 5% APR\n    uint256 internal constant MIN_DEBT = 100e6; // 100 USDC\n    uint256 internal constant ETH_HEARTBEAT = 1 hours;\n    uint256 internal constant USDC_HEARTBEAT = 1 days;\n\n    function setUp() public {\n        vm.warp(1_700_000_000);\n\n        weth = new MockERC20(\"Wrapped Ether\", \"WETH\", 18);\n        usdc = new MockERC20(\"USD Coin\", \"USDC\", 6);\n        ethFeed = new MockAggregator(8, 2000e8);\n        usdcFeed = new MockAggregator(8, 1e8);\n\n        pool = new LendingPool(\n            owner,\n            address(weth),\n            address(usdc),\n            address(ethFeed),\n            address(usdcFeed),\n            RATE_BPS,\n            MIN_DEBT,\n            ETH_HEARTBEAT,\n            USDC_HEARTBEAT\n        );\n\n        // Operator seeds borrowable liquidity.\n        usdc.mint(owner, 1_000_000e6);\n        vm.startPrank(owner);\n        usdc.approve(address(pool), type(uint256).max);\n        pool.fund(1_000_000e6);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        weth.mint(alice, 100e18);\n        usdc.mint(alice, 100_000e6);\n        usdc.mint(keeper, 100_000e6);\n\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        weth.approve(address(pool), type(uint256).max);\n        usdc.approve(address(pool), type(uint256).max);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        vm.prank(keeper);\n        usdc.approve(address(pool), type(uint256).max);\n    }\n\n    /* ------------------------------ helpers ------------------------------ */\n\n    function _deposit(address who, uint256 amount) internal {\n        vm.prank(who);\n        pool.depositCollateral(amount, who);\n    }\n\n    function _borrow(address who, uint256 amount) internal {\n        vm.prank(who);\n        pool.borrow(amount, who);\n    }\n\n    /* ------------------------------- setup ------------------------------- */\n\n    function test_ConstructorReadsDecimalsFromTokens() public view {\n        assertEq(pool.collateralUnit(), 1e18, \"weth unit\");\n        assertEq(pool.debtUnit(), 1e6, \"usdc unit\");\n        assertEq(pool.collateralPrice(), 2000e18, \"eth price normalised to 18dp\");\n        assertEq(pool.debtPrice(), 1e18, \"usdc price normalised to 18dp\");\n        assertEq(pool.borrowIndex(), pool.RAY());\n    }\n\n    function test_RevertConstructorZeroAddress() public {\n        vm.expectRevert(LendingPool.ZeroAddress.selector);\n        new LendingPool(\n            owner, address(0), address(usdc), address(ethFeed), address(usdcFeed), RATE_BPS, MIN_DEBT, 1 hours, 1 days\n        );\n    }\n\n    function test_RevertConstructorRateAboveCap() public {\n        vm.expectRevert(LendingPool.InvalidParameter.selector);\n        new LendingPool(\n            owner,\n            address(weth),\n            address(usdc),\n            address(ethFeed),\n            address(usdcFeed),\n            5001,\n            MIN_DEBT,\n            1 hours,\n            1 days\n        );\n    }\n\n    /* --------------------------- borrower flow --------------------------- */\n\n    function test_DepositBorrowRepayWithdraw() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        assertEq(pool.collateralOf(alice), 10e18);\n\n        // 10 WETH @ $2000 = $20,000 -> 70% LTV = $14,000 = 14,000 USDC (6 decimals).\n        assertEq(pool.maxBorrowable(alice), 14_000e6, \"borrow power in USDC units\");\n\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(alice), 114_000e6);\n        assertEq(pool.debtOf(alice), 14_000e6);\n        assertFalse(pool.isLiquidatable(alice));\n\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        uint256 repaid = pool.repay(type(uint256).max, alice);\n        assertEq(repaid, 14_000e6);\n        assertEq(pool.debtOf(alice), 0, \"debt fully cleared, no dust\");\n        assertEq(pool.scaledDebtOf(alice), 0);\n        assertEq(pool.totalScaledDebt(), 0);\n\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        pool.withdrawCollateral(10e18, alice);\n        assertEq(weth.balanceOf(alice), 100e18);\n    }\n\n    function test_BorrowAtExactlyMaxLtvSucceeds() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n        assertEq(pool.debtOf(alice), 14_000e6);\n    }\n\n    function test_RevertBorrowOneUnitAboveMaxLtv() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(\n            abi.encodeWithSelector(LendingPool.ExceedsBorrowPower.selector, 14_000e18 + 1e12, 14_000e18)\n        );\n        pool.borrow(14_000e6 + 1, alice);\n    }\n\n    function test_RevertBorrowBeyondLiquidity() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 100e18); // $200k collateral, more borrow power than the pool holds\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(abi.encodeWithSelector(LendingPool.InsufficientLiquidity.selector, 1_000_001e6, 1_000_000e6));\n        pool.borrow(1_000_001e6, alice);\n    }\n\n    function test_RevertBorrowBelowMinDebt() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(abi.encodeWithSelector(LendingPool.DebtBelowMinimum.selector, 1e6, MIN_DEBT));\n        pool.borrow(1e6, alice);\n    }\n\n    function test_RevertWithdrawThatBreaksLtv() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(abi.encodeWithSelector(LendingPool.ExceedsBorrowPower.selector, 14_000e18, 13_986e18));\n        pool.withdrawCollateral(0.01e18, alice);\n    }\n\n    function test_PartialWithdrawWhileHealthy() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 7_000e6); // 50% of the $10k needed; plenty of headroom\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        pool.withdrawCollateral(4e18, alice);\n        assertEq(pool.collateralOf(alice), 6e18);\n        assertFalse(pool.isLiquidatable(alice));\n    }\n\n    function test_RepayLeavingDustReverts() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(abi.encodeWithSelector(LendingPool.DebtBelowMinimum.selector, 50e6, MIN_DEBT));\n        pool.repay(14_000e6 - 50e6, alice);\n    }\n\n    function test_ThirdPartyCanRepayOnBehalf() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n        vm.prank(keeper);\n        pool.repay(14_000e6, alice);\n        assertEq(pool.debtOf(alice), 0);\n    }\n\n    /* ------------------------------ interest ----------------------------- */\n\n    function test_InterestAccruesAtFlatRate() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 10_000e6);\n\n        vm.warp(block.timestamp + 365 days);\n        assertEq(pool.debtOf(alice), 10_500e6, \"5% APR over one year\");\n\n        pool.accrue();\n        assertEq(pool.borrowIndex(), 1.05e27);\n    }\n\n    function test_NoRetroactiveInterestOnIdlePool() public {\n        vm.warp(block.timestamp + 365 days); // nothing borrowed during this window\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 10_000e6);\n        assertEq(pool.debtOf(alice), 10_000e6);\n        assertEq(pool.borrowIndex(), pool.RAY(), \"index untouched while debt was zero\");\n    }\n\n    function test_RateChangeAccruesOldRateFirst() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 10_000e6);\n        vm.warp(block.timestamp + 365 days);\n\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        pool.setBorrowRate(1000);\n        assertEq(pool.debtOf(alice), 10_500e6, \"old rate applied to elapsed time\");\n\n        vm.warp(block.timestamp + 365 days);\n        assertEq(pool.debtOf(alice), 11_550e6, \"new rate applies going forward\");\n    }\n\n    function test_InterestCanPushPositionIntoLiquidation() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6); // exactly 70% LTV\n        assertFalse(pool.isLiquidatable(alice));\n\n        // $17,000 is the 85% line; 14,000 * 1.05^k crosses it during year 5.\n        vm.warp(block.timestamp + 5 * 365 days);\n        assertTrue(pool.isLiquidatable(alice), \"interest alone makes it liquidatable\");\n    }\n\n    /* ---------------------------- liquidation ---------------------------- */\n\n    function test_RevertLiquidateHealthyPosition() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n\n        vm.prank(keeper);\n        vm.expectRevert(abi.encodeWithSelector(LendingPool.PositionHealthy.selector, 14_000e18, 17_000e18));\n        pool.liquidate(alice, 1_000e6, 0);\n    }\n\n    function test_LiquidatePaysFivePercentBonus() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n\n        ethFeed.setAnswer(1600e8); // collateral $16,000, 85% line = $13,600 < $14,000 debt\n        assertTrue(pool.isLiquidatable(alice));\n\n        uint256 maxRepay = 7_000e6; // 50% close factor\n        // $7,000 * 1.05 = $7,350 of WETH at $1,600 = 4.59375 WETH\n        uint256 expectedSeize = 4.59375e18;\n\n        (uint256 previewRepaid, uint256 previewSeized) = pool.previewLiquidation(alice, type(uint256).max);\n        assertEq(previewRepaid, maxRepay);\n        assertEq(previewSeized, expectedSeize);\n\n        uint256 keeperWethBefore = weth.balanceOf(keeper);\n        uint256 keeperUsdcBefore = usdc.balanceOf(keeper);\n\n        vm.prank(keeper);\n        (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized) = pool.liquidate(alice, type(uint256).max, expectedSeize);\n\n        assertEq(repaid, maxRepay, \"capped at close factor\");\n        assertEq(seized, expectedSeize, \"repaid value + 5%\");\n        assertEq(weth.balanceOf(keeper) - keeperWethBefore, expectedSeize);\n        assertEq(keeperUsdcBefore - usdc.balanceOf(keeper), maxRepay);\n        assertEq(pool.debtOf(alice), 7_000e6);\n        assertEq(pool.collateralOf(alice), 10e18 - expectedSeize);\n    }\n\n    function test_LiquidationRestoresHealth() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n        ethFeed.setAnswer(1600e8);\n\n        vm.prank(keeper);\n        pool.liquidate(alice, type(uint256).max, 0);\n        assertFalse(pool.isLiquidatable(alice), \"position back under the 85% line\");\n    }\n\n    function test_LiquidationRespectsSlippageGuard() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n        ethFeed.setAnswer(1600e8);\n\n        vm.prank(keeper);\n        vm.expectRevert(abi.encodeWithSelector(LendingPool.SlippageExceeded.selector, 4.59375e18, 5e18));\n        pool.liquidate(alice, type(uint256).max, 5e18);\n    }\n\n    function test_PartialLiquidationBelowCloseFactor() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n        ethFeed.setAnswer(1600e8);\n\n        vm.prank(keeper);\n        (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized) = pool.liquidate(alice, 1_000e6, 0);\n        assertEq(repaid, 1_000e6);\n        assertEq(seized, (1_000e18 * 105 / 100) * 1e18 / 1600e18);\n        assertEq(pool.debtOf(alice), 13_000e6);\n    }\n\n    function test_UnderwaterLiquidationSeizesAllAndLeavesBadDebt() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n\n        ethFeed.setAnswer(700e8); // collateral now worth $7,000 against $14,000 of debt\n\n        vm.prank(keeper);\n        (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized) = pool.liquidate(alice, type(uint256).max, 0);\n\n        assertEq(seized, 10e18, \"all collateral gone\");\n        assertEq(pool.collateralOf(alice), 0);\n        // $7,000 of collateral covers $7,000 / 1.05 = $6,666.666666 of debt.\n        assertEq(repaid, 6_666_666_666);\n        assertEq(pool.debtOf(alice), 14_000e6 - 6_666_666_666, \"residual is bad debt\");\n    }\n\n    function test_LiquidationClosesFullyWhenRemainderWouldBeDust() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 1e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 150e6); // just above the 100 USDC floor\n        ethFeed.setAnswer(170e8); // $170 collateral vs $150 debt -> 85% line is $144.50\n\n        assertTrue(pool.isLiquidatable(alice));\n        vm.prank(keeper);\n        (uint256 repaid,) = pool.liquidate(alice, type(uint256).max, 0);\n        assertEq(repaid, 150e6, \"close factor lifted to avoid leaving 75 USDC of dust\");\n        assertEq(pool.debtOf(alice), 0);\n    }\n\n    function test_RevertLiquidateNoDebt() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        vm.prank(keeper);\n        vm.expectRevert(LendingPool.NoDebt.selector);\n        pool.liquidate(alice, 1e6, 0);\n    }\n\n    /* ------------------------------- oracle ------------------------------ */\n\n    function test_RevertOnStalePrice() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        ethFeed.setStaleness(ETH_HEARTBEAT + 1);\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(\n            abi.encodeWithSelector(\n                LendingPool.PriceStale.selector, block.timestamp - ETH_HEARTBEAT - 1, ETH_HEARTBEAT\n            )\n        );\n        pool.borrow(1_000e6, alice);\n    }\n\n    function test_RevertOnNonPositivePrice() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        ethFeed.setAnswer(0);\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(LendingPool.PriceNotPositive.selector);\n        pool.borrow(1_000e6, alice);\n\n        ethFeed.setAnswer(-1);\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(LendingPool.PriceNotPositive.selector);\n        pool.borrow(1_000e6, alice);\n    }\n\n    function test_RevertOnIncompleteRound() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        ethFeed.setAnsweredInRound(0);\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(LendingPool.PriceRoundIncomplete.selector);\n        pool.borrow(1_000e6, alice);\n\n        ethFeed.setAnsweredInRound(ethFeed.roundId());\n        ethFeed.setZeroStartedAt(true);\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(LendingPool.PriceRoundIncomplete.selector);\n        pool.borrow(1_000e6, alice);\n    }\n\n    function test_DebtFeedDepegRaisesDebtValue() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n        assertFalse(pool.isLiquidatable(alice));\n\n        usdcFeed.setAnswer(1.25e8); // USDC trades at $1.25 -> debt worth $17,500 > $17,000\n        assertTrue(pool.isLiquidatable(alice), \"debt-side depeg is priced in, not assumed away\");\n    }\n\n    /* --------------------------- access control -------------------------- */\n\n    function test_OnlyOwnerFunctions() public {\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        bytes memory err = abi.encodeWithSelector(Ownable.OwnableUnauthorizedAccount.selector, alice);\n\n        vm.expectRevert(err);\n        pool.setBorrowRate(100);\n        vm.expectRevert(err);\n        pool.setMinDebt(1e6);\n        vm.expectRevert(err);\n        pool.setStalenessWindows(1 hours, 1 hours);\n        vm.expectRevert(err);\n        pool.pause();\n        vm.expectRevert(err);\n        pool.withdrawReserves(1e6, alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(err);\n        pool.sweep(address(weth), alice, 1);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_RevertSetBorrowRateAboveCap() public {\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        vm.expectRevert(LendingPool.InvalidParameter.selector);\n        pool.setBorrowRate(5001);\n    }\n\n    function test_RevertSweepOfMarketAssets() public {\n        vm.startPrank(owner);\n        vm.expectRevert(LendingPool.InvalidParameter.selector);\n        pool.sweep(address(weth), owner, 1);\n        vm.expectRevert(LendingPool.InvalidParameter.selector);\n        pool.sweep(address(usdc), owner, 1);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_WithdrawReservesBoundedByIdleLiquidity() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 100e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 900_000e6);\n\n        vm.startPrank(owner);\n        vm.expectRevert(abi.encodeWithSelector(LendingPool.InsufficientLiquidity.selector, 200_000e6, 100_000e6));\n        pool.withdrawReserves(200_000e6, owner);\n        pool.withdrawReserves(100_000e6, owner);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n        assertEq(pool.availableLiquidity(), 0);\n    }\n\n    function test_OwnershipTransferIsTwoStep() public {\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        pool.transferOwnership(alice);\n        assertEq(pool.owner(), owner, \"not transferred until accepted\");\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        pool.acceptOwnership();\n        assertEq(pool.owner(), alice);\n    }\n\n    /* ------------------------------- pausing ----------------------------- */\n\n    function test_PauseBlocksBorrowingButNotRepayment() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        pool.pause();\n\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(Pausable.EnforcedPause.selector);\n        pool.borrow(1e6, alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(Pausable.EnforcedPause.selector);\n        pool.depositCollateral(1e18, alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(Pausable.EnforcedPause.selector);\n        pool.withdrawCollateral(1e18, alice);\n\n        // Repayment stays open so borrowers are never trapped.\n        pool.repay(type(uint256).max, alice);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n        assertEq(pool.debtOf(alice), 0);\n    }\n\n    /* ----------------------------- reentrancy ---------------------------- */\n\n    function test_ReentrantCollateralTokenIsBlocked() public {\n        ReentrantToken evil = new ReentrantToken();\n        MockAggregator evilFeed = new MockAggregator(8, 2000e8);\n        LendingPool evilPool = new LendingPool(\n            owner, address(evil), address(usdc), address(evilFeed), address(usdcFeed), RATE_BPS, MIN_DEBT, 1 hours, 1 days\n        );\n        evil.setPool(address(evilPool));\n        evil.mint(address(this), 10e18);\n        evil.approve(address(evilPool), type(uint256).max);\n        evilPool.depositCollateral(10e18, address(this));\n\n        evil.setAttacking(true);\n        evilPool.withdrawCollateral(5e18, address(this));\n\n        assertTrue(evil.reenterAttempted(), \"callback fired\");\n        assertEq(\n            bytes4(evil.reenterError()),\n            ReentrancyGuard.ReentrancyGuardReentrantCall.selector,\n            \"re-entry rejected by the guard\"\n        );\n        assertEq(evilPool.collateralOf(address(this)), 5e18, \"accounting unchanged by the attempt\");\n    }\n\n    function test_FeeOnTransferCollateralCreditsOnlyWhatArrived() public {\n        // The pool credits the observed balance delta, so a deflationary token can never be\n        // credited for more collateral than the pool actually holds.\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        assertEq(pool.collateralOf(alice), weth.balanceOf(address(pool)));\n    }\n\n    /* ------------------------------- fuzzing ----------------------------- */\n\n    /// @dev The pool must always hold at least the collateral it has credited to borrowers.\n    function testFuzz_CollateralAccountingNeverExceedsBalance(\n        uint256 depositAmount,\n        uint256 borrowAmount,\n        uint256 elapsed\n    ) public {\n        depositAmount = bound(depositAmount, 0.001e18, 100e18);\n        elapsed = bound(elapsed, 0, 3650 days);\n\n        _deposit(alice, depositAmount);\n        uint256 maxBorrow = pool.maxBorrowable(alice);\n        vm.assume(maxBorrow >= MIN_DEBT);\n        borrowAmount = bound(borrowAmount, MIN_DEBT, maxBorrow);\n        _borrow(alice, borrowAmount);\n\n        vm.warp(block.timestamp + elapsed);\n        pool.accrue();\n\n        assertGe(weth.balanceOf(address(pool)), pool.collateralOf(alice));\n        assertGe(pool.debtOf(alice), borrowAmount, \"debt never shrinks on its own\");\n    }\n\n    /// @dev A liquidator's payout is always the repaid value plus the bonus, and never more\n    ///      collateral than the borrower had.\n    function testFuzz_LiquidationBonusIsBounded(uint256 priceDrop, uint256 repayAmount) public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n\n        // Any price in [$1,000, $1,646] leaves the position liquidatable but not yet worthless.\n        uint256 newPrice = bound(priceDrop, 1000e8, 1646e8);\n        ethFeed.setAnswer(int256(newPrice));\n        vm.assume(pool.isLiquidatable(alice));\n\n        repayAmount = bound(repayAmount, 1e6, 14_000e6);\n        uint256 collateralBefore = pool.collateralOf(alice);\n\n        vm.prank(keeper);\n        (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized) = pool.liquidate(alice, repayAmount, 0);\n\n        assertLe(repaid, 7_000e6, \"close factor holds\");\n        assertLe(seized, collateralBefore, \"cannot seize more than posted\");\n\n        uint256 repaidValue = repaid * 1e12; // USDC 6dp -> 18dp USD, USDC pegged at $1 here\n        uint256 seizedValue = seized * newPrice * 1e10 / 1e18;\n        assertLe(seizedValue, repaidValue * 105 / 100, \"bonus never exceeds 5%\");\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

      _borrow(alice, 900_000e6);","originalFile":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity 0.8.28;\n\nimport {Test} from \"forge-std/Test.sol\";\nimport {IERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol\";\nimport {Ownable} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol\";\nimport {Pausable} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/Pausable.sol\";\nimport {ReentrancyGuard} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/ReentrancyGuard.sol\";\n\nimport {LendingPool} from \"../src/LendingPool.sol\";\nimport {MockERC20} from \"./mocks/MockERC20.sol\";\nimport {MockAggregator} from \"./mocks/MockAggregator.sol\";\nimport {ReentrantToken} from \"./mocks/ReentrantToken.sol\";\n\ncontract LendingPoolTest is Test {\n    LendingPool internal pool;\n    MockERC20 internal weth;\n    MockERC20 internal usdc;\n    MockAggregator internal ethFeed;\n    MockAggregator internal usdcFeed;\n\n    address internal owner = address(0xA11CE);\n    address internal alice = address(0xB0B);\n    address internal keeper = address(0xC0FFEE);\n\n    uint256 internal constant RATE_BPS = 500; // 5% APR\n    uint256 internal constant MIN_DEBT = 100e6; // 100 USDC\n    uint256 internal constant ETH_HEARTBEAT = 1 hours;\n    uint256 internal constant USDC_HEARTBEAT = 1 days;\n\n    function setUp() public {\n        vm.warp(1_700_000_000);\n\n        weth = new MockERC20(\"Wrapped Ether\", \"WETH\", 18);\n        usdc = new MockERC20(\"USD Coin\", \"USDC\", 6);\n        ethFeed = new MockAggregator(8, 2000e8);\n        usdcFeed = new MockAggregator(8, 1e8);\n\n        pool = new LendingPool(\n            owner,\n            address(weth),\n            address(usdc),\n            address(ethFeed),\n            address(usdcFeed),\n            RATE_BPS,\n            MIN_DEBT,\n            ETH_HEARTBEAT,\n            USDC_HEARTBEAT\n        );\n\n        // Operator seeds borrowable liquidity.\n        usdc.mint(owner, 1_000_000e6);\n        vm.startPrank(owner);\n        usdc.approve(address(pool), type(uint256).max);\n        pool.fund(1_000_000e6);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        weth.mint(alice, 100e18);\n        usdc.mint(alice, 100_000e6);\n        usdc.mint(keeper, 100_000e6);\n\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        weth.approve(address(pool), type(uint256).max);\n        usdc.approve(address(pool), type(uint256).max);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n\n        vm.prank(keeper);\n        usdc.approve(address(pool), type(uint256).max);\n    }\n\n    /* ------------------------------ helpers ------------------------------ */\n\n    function _deposit(address who, uint256 amount) internal {\n        vm.prank(who);\n        pool.depositCollateral(amount, who);\n    }\n\n    function _borrow(address who, uint256 amount) internal {\n        vm.prank(who);\n        pool.borrow(amount, who);\n    }\n\n    /* ------------------------------- setup ------------------------------- */\n\n    function test_ConstructorReadsDecimalsFromTokens() public view {\n        assertEq(pool.collateralUnit(), 1e18, \"weth unit\");\n        assertEq(pool.debtUnit(), 1e6, \"usdc unit\");\n        assertEq(pool.collateralPrice(), 2000e18, \"eth price normalised to 18dp\");\n        assertEq(pool.debtPrice(), 1e18, \"usdc price normalised to 18dp\");\n        assertEq(pool.borrowIndex(), pool.RAY());\n    }\n\n    function test_RevertConstructorZeroAddress() public {\n        vm.expectRevert(LendingPool.ZeroAddress.selector);\n        new LendingPool(\n            owner, address(0), address(usdc), address(ethFeed), address(usdcFeed), RATE_BPS, MIN_DEBT, 1 hours, 1 days\n        );\n    }\n\n    function test_RevertConstructorRateAboveCap() public {\n        vm.expectRevert(LendingPool.InvalidParameter.selector);\n        new LendingPool(\n            owner,\n            address(weth),\n            address(usdc),\n            address(ethFeed),\n            address(usdcFeed),\n            5001,\n            MIN_DEBT,\n            1 hours,\n            1 days\n        );\n    }\n\n    /* --------------------------- borrower flow --------------------------- */\n\n    function test_DepositBorrowRepayWithdraw() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        assertEq(pool.collateralOf(alice), 10e18);\n\n        // 10 WETH @ $2000 = $20,000 -> 70% LTV = $14,000 = 14,000 USDC (6 decimals).\n        assertEq(pool.maxBorrowable(alice), 14_000e6, \"borrow power in USDC units\");\n\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n        assertEq(usdc.balanceOf(alice), 114_000e6);\n        assertEq(pool.debtOf(alice), 14_000e6);\n        assertFalse(pool.isLiquidatable(alice));\n\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        uint256 repaid = pool.repay(type(uint256).max, alice);\n        assertEq(repaid, 14_000e6);\n        assertEq(pool.debtOf(alice), 0, \"debt fully cleared, no dust\");\n        assertEq(pool.scaledDebtOf(alice), 0);\n        assertEq(pool.totalScaledDebt(), 0);\n\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        pool.withdrawCollateral(10e18, alice);\n        assertEq(weth.balanceOf(alice), 100e18);\n    }\n\n    function test_BorrowAtExactlyMaxLtvSucceeds() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n        assertEq(pool.debtOf(alice), 14_000e6);\n    }\n\n    function test_RevertBorrowOneUnitAboveMaxLtv() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(\n            abi.encodeWithSelector(LendingPool.ExceedsBorrowPower.selector, 14_000e18 + 1e12, 14_000e18)\n        );\n        pool.borrow(14_000e6 + 1, alice);\n    }\n\n    function test_RevertBorrowBeyondLiquidity() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 100e18); // $200k collateral, more borrow power than the pool holds\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(abi.encodeWithSelector(LendingPool.InsufficientLiquidity.selector, 1_000_001e6, 1_000_000e6));\n        pool.borrow(1_000_001e6, alice);\n    }\n\n    function test_RevertBorrowBelowMinDebt() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(abi.encodeWithSelector(LendingPool.DebtBelowMinimum.selector, 1e6, MIN_DEBT));\n        pool.borrow(1e6, alice);\n    }\n\n    function test_RevertWithdrawThatBreaksLtv() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(abi.encodeWithSelector(LendingPool.ExceedsBorrowPower.selector, 14_000e18, 13_986e18));\n        pool.withdrawCollateral(0.01e18, alice);\n    }\n\n    function test_PartialWithdrawWhileHealthy() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 7_000e6); // 50% of the $10k needed; plenty of headroom\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        pool.withdrawCollateral(4e18, alice);\n        assertEq(pool.collateralOf(alice), 6e18);\n        assertFalse(pool.isLiquidatable(alice));\n    }\n\n    function test_RepayLeavingDustReverts() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(abi.encodeWithSelector(LendingPool.DebtBelowMinimum.selector, 50e6, MIN_DEBT));\n        pool.repay(14_000e6 - 50e6, alice);\n    }\n\n    function test_ThirdPartyCanRepayOnBehalf() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n        vm.prank(keeper);\n        pool.repay(14_000e6, alice);\n        assertEq(pool.debtOf(alice), 0);\n    }\n\n    /* ------------------------------ interest ----------------------------- */\n\n    function test_InterestAccruesAtFlatRate() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 10_000e6);\n\n        vm.warp(block.timestamp + 365 days);\n        assertEq(pool.debtOf(alice), 10_500e6, \"5% APR over one year\");\n\n        pool.accrue();\n        assertEq(pool.borrowIndex(), 1.05e27);\n    }\n\n    function test_NoRetroactiveInterestOnIdlePool() public {\n        vm.warp(block.timestamp + 365 days); // nothing borrowed during this window\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 10_000e6);\n        assertEq(pool.debtOf(alice), 10_000e6);\n        assertEq(pool.borrowIndex(), pool.RAY(), \"index untouched while debt was zero\");\n    }\n\n    function test_RateChangeAccruesOldRateFirst() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 10_000e6);\n        vm.warp(block.timestamp + 365 days);\n\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        pool.setBorrowRate(1000);\n        assertEq(pool.debtOf(alice), 10_500e6, \"old rate applied to elapsed time\");\n\n        vm.warp(block.timestamp + 365 days);\n        assertEq(pool.debtOf(alice), 11_550e6, \"new rate applies going forward\");\n    }\n\n    function test_InterestCanPushPositionIntoLiquidation() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6); // exactly 70% LTV\n        assertFalse(pool.isLiquidatable(alice));\n\n        // $17,000 is the 85% line; 14,000 * 1.05^k crosses it during year 5.\n        vm.warp(block.timestamp + 5 * 365 days);\n        assertTrue(pool.isLiquidatable(alice), \"interest alone makes it liquidatable\");\n    }\n\n    /* ---------------------------- liquidation ---------------------------- */\n\n    function test_RevertLiquidateHealthyPosition() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n\n        vm.prank(keeper);\n        vm.expectRevert(abi.encodeWithSelector(LendingPool.PositionHealthy.selector, 14_000e18, 17_000e18));\n        pool.liquidate(alice, 1_000e6, 0);\n    }\n\n    function test_LiquidatePaysFivePercentBonus() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n\n        ethFeed.setAnswer(1600e8); // collateral $16,000, 85% line = $13,600 < $14,000 debt\n        assertTrue(pool.isLiquidatable(alice));\n\n        uint256 maxRepay = 7_000e6; // 50% close factor\n        // $7,000 * 1.05 = $7,350 of WETH at $1,600 = 4.59375 WETH\n        uint256 expectedSeize = 4.59375e18;\n\n        (uint256 previewRepaid, uint256 previewSeized) = pool.previewLiquidation(alice, type(uint256).max);\n        assertEq(previewRepaid, maxRepay);\n        assertEq(previewSeized, expectedSeize);\n\n        uint256 keeperWethBefore = weth.balanceOf(keeper);\n        uint256 keeperUsdcBefore = usdc.balanceOf(keeper);\n\n        vm.prank(keeper);\n        (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized) = pool.liquidate(alice, type(uint256).max, expectedSeize);\n\n        assertEq(repaid, maxRepay, \"capped at close factor\");\n        assertEq(seized, expectedSeize, \"repaid value + 5%\");\n        assertEq(weth.balanceOf(keeper) - keeperWethBefore, expectedSeize);\n        assertEq(keeperUsdcBefore - usdc.balanceOf(keeper), maxRepay);\n        assertEq(pool.debtOf(alice), 7_000e6);\n        assertEq(pool.collateralOf(alice), 10e18 - expectedSeize);\n    }\n\n    function test_LiquidationRestoresHealth() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n        ethFeed.setAnswer(1600e8);\n\n        vm.prank(keeper);\n        pool.liquidate(alice, type(uint256).max, 0);\n        assertFalse(pool.isLiquidatable(alice), \"position back under the 85% line\");\n    }\n\n    function test_LiquidationRespectsSlippageGuard() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n        ethFeed.setAnswer(1600e8);\n\n        vm.prank(keeper);\n        vm.expectRevert(abi.encodeWithSelector(LendingPool.SlippageExceeded.selector, 4.59375e18, 5e18));\n        pool.liquidate(alice, type(uint256).max, 5e18);\n    }\n\n    function test_PartialLiquidationBelowCloseFactor() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n        ethFeed.setAnswer(1600e8);\n\n        vm.prank(keeper);\n        (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized) = pool.liquidate(alice, 1_000e6, 0);\n        assertEq(repaid, 1_000e6);\n        assertEq(seized, (1_000e18 * 105 / 100) * 1e18 / 1600e18);\n        assertEq(pool.debtOf(alice), 13_000e6);\n    }\n\n    function test_UnderwaterLiquidationSeizesAllAndLeavesBadDebt() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n\n        ethFeed.setAnswer(700e8); // collateral now worth $7,000 against $14,000 of debt\n\n        vm.prank(keeper);\n        (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized) = pool.liquidate(alice, type(uint256).max, 0);\n\n        assertEq(seized, 10e18, \"all collateral gone\");\n        assertEq(pool.collateralOf(alice), 0);\n        // $7,000 of collateral covers $7,000 / 1.05 = $6,666.666666 of debt.\n        assertEq(repaid, 6_666_666_666);\n        assertEq(pool.debtOf(alice), 14_000e6 - 6_666_666_666, \"residual is bad debt\");\n    }\n\n    function test_LiquidationClosesFullyWhenRemainderWouldBeDust() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 1e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 150e6); // just above the 100 USDC floor\n        ethFeed.setAnswer(170e8); // $170 collateral vs $150 debt -> 85% line is $144.50\n\n        assertTrue(pool.isLiquidatable(alice));\n        vm.prank(keeper);\n        (uint256 repaid,) = pool.liquidate(alice, type(uint256).max, 0);\n        assertEq(repaid, 150e6, \"close factor lifted to avoid leaving 75 USDC of dust\");\n        assertEq(pool.debtOf(alice), 0);\n    }\n\n    function test_RevertLiquidateNoDebt() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        vm.prank(keeper);\n        vm.expectRevert(LendingPool.NoDebt.selector);\n        pool.liquidate(alice, 1e6, 0);\n    }\n\n    /* ------------------------------- oracle ------------------------------ */\n\n    function test_RevertOnStalePrice() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        ethFeed.setStaleness(ETH_HEARTBEAT + 1);\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(\n            abi.encodeWithSelector(\n                LendingPool.PriceStale.selector, block.timestamp - ETH_HEARTBEAT - 1, ETH_HEARTBEAT\n            )\n        );\n        pool.borrow(1_000e6, alice);\n    }\n\n    function test_RevertOnNonPositivePrice() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        ethFeed.setAnswer(0);\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(LendingPool.PriceNotPositive.selector);\n        pool.borrow(1_000e6, alice);\n\n        ethFeed.setAnswer(-1);\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(LendingPool.PriceNotPositive.selector);\n        pool.borrow(1_000e6, alice);\n    }\n\n    function test_RevertOnIncompleteRound() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        ethFeed.setAnsweredInRound(0);\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(LendingPool.PriceRoundIncomplete.selector);\n        pool.borrow(1_000e6, alice);\n\n        ethFeed.setAnsweredInRound(ethFeed.roundId());\n        ethFeed.setZeroStartedAt(true);\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(LendingPool.PriceRoundIncomplete.selector);\n        pool.borrow(1_000e6, alice);\n    }\n\n    function test_DebtFeedDepegRaisesDebtValue() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n        assertFalse(pool.isLiquidatable(alice));\n\n        usdcFeed.setAnswer(1.25e8); // USDC trades at $1.25 -> debt worth $17,500 > $17,000\n        assertTrue(pool.isLiquidatable(alice), \"debt-side depeg is priced in, not assumed away\");\n    }\n\n    /* --------------------------- access control -------------------------- */\n\n    function test_OnlyOwnerFunctions() public {\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        bytes memory err = abi.encodeWithSelector(Ownable.OwnableUnauthorizedAccount.selector, alice);\n\n        vm.expectRevert(err);\n        pool.setBorrowRate(100);\n        vm.expectRevert(err);\n        pool.setMinDebt(1e6);\n        vm.expectRevert(err);\n        pool.setStalenessWindows(1 hours, 1 hours);\n        vm.expectRevert(err);\n        pool.pause();\n        vm.expectRevert(err);\n        pool.withdrawReserves(1e6, alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(err);\n        pool.sweep(address(weth), alice, 1);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_RevertSetBorrowRateAboveCap() public {\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        vm.expectRevert(LendingPool.InvalidParameter.selector);\n        pool.setBorrowRate(5001);\n    }\n\n    function test_RevertSweepOfMarketAssets() public {\n        vm.startPrank(owner);\n        vm.expectRevert(LendingPool.InvalidParameter.selector);\n        pool.sweep(address(weth), owner, 1);\n        vm.expectRevert(LendingPool.InvalidParameter.selector);\n        pool.sweep(address(usdc), owner, 1);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n    }\n\n    function test_WithdrawReservesBoundedByIdleLiquidity() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 100e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 900_000e6);\n\n        vm.startPrank(owner);\n        vm.expectRevert(abi.encodeWithSelector(LendingPool.InsufficientLiquidity.selector, 200_000e6, 100_000e6));\n        pool.withdrawReserves(200_000e6, owner);\n        pool.withdrawReserves(100_000e6, owner);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n        assertEq(pool.availableLiquidity(), 0);\n    }\n\n    function test_OwnershipTransferIsTwoStep() public {\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        pool.transferOwnership(alice);\n        assertEq(pool.owner(), owner, \"not transferred until accepted\");\n        vm.prank(alice);\n        pool.acceptOwnership();\n        assertEq(pool.owner(), alice);\n    }\n\n    /* ------------------------------- pausing ----------------------------- */\n\n    function test_PauseBlocksBorrowingButNotRepayment() public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n\n        vm.prank(owner);\n        pool.pause();\n\n        vm.startPrank(alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(Pausable.EnforcedPause.selector);\n        pool.borrow(1e6, alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(Pausable.EnforcedPause.selector);\n        pool.depositCollateral(1e18, alice);\n        vm.expectRevert(Pausable.EnforcedPause.selector);\n        pool.withdrawCollateral(1e18, alice);\n\n        // Repayment stays open so borrowers are never trapped.\n        pool.repay(type(uint256).max, alice);\n        vm.stopPrank();\n        assertEq(pool.debtOf(alice), 0);\n    }\n\n    /* ----------------------------- reentrancy ---------------------------- */\n\n    function test_ReentrantCollateralTokenIsBlocked() public {\n        ReentrantToken evil = new ReentrantToken();\n        MockAggregator evilFeed = new MockAggregator(8, 2000e8);\n        LendingPool evilPool = new LendingPool(\n            owner, address(evil), address(usdc), address(evilFeed), address(usdcFeed), RATE_BPS, MIN_DEBT, 1 hours, 1 days\n        );\n        evil.setPool(address(evilPool));\n        evil.mint(address(this), 10e18);\n        evil.approve(address(evilPool), type(uint256).max);\n        evilPool.depositCollateral(10e18, address(this));\n\n        evil.setAttacking(true);\n        evilPool.withdrawCollateral(5e18, address(this));\n\n        assertTrue(evil.reenterAttempted(), \"callback fired\");\n        assertEq(\n            bytes4(evil.reenterError()),\n            ReentrancyGuard.ReentrancyGuardReentrantCall.selector,\n            \"re-entry rejected by the guard\"\n        );\n        assertEq(evilPool.collateralOf(address(this)), 5e18, \"accounting unchanged by the attempt\");\n    }\n\n    function test_FeeOnTransferCollateralCreditsOnlyWhatArrived() public {\n        // The pool credits the observed balance delta, so a deflationary token can never be\n        // credited for more collateral than the pool actually holds.\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        assertEq(pool.collateralOf(alice), weth.balanceOf(address(pool)));\n    }\n\n    /* ------------------------------- fuzzing ----------------------------- */\n\n    /// @dev The pool must always hold at least the collateral it has credited to borrowers.\n    function testFuzz_CollateralAccountingNeverExceedsBalance(\n        uint256 depositAmount,\n        uint256 borrowAmount,\n        uint256 elapsed\n    ) public {\n        depositAmount = bound(depositAmount, 0.001e18, 100e18);\n        elapsed = bound(elapsed, 0, 3650 days);\n\n        _deposit(alice, depositAmount);\n        uint256 maxBorrow = pool.maxBorrowable(alice);\n        vm.assume(maxBorrow >= MIN_DEBT);\n        borrowAmount = bound(borrowAmount, MIN_DEBT, maxBorrow);\n        _borrow(alice, borrowAmount);\n\n        vm.warp(block.timestamp + elapsed);\n        pool.accrue();\n\n        assertGe(weth.balanceOf(address(pool)), pool.collateralOf(alice));\n        assertGe(pool.debtOf(alice), borrowAmount, \"debt never shrinks on its own\");\n    }\n\n    /// @dev A liquidator's payout is always the repaid value plus the bonus, and never more\n    ///      collateral than the borrower had.\n    function testFuzz_LiquidationBonusIsBounded(uint256 priceDrop, uint256 repayAmount) public {\n        _deposit(alice, 10e18);\n        _borrow(alice, 14_000e6);\n\n        // Any price in [$1,000, $1,646] leaves the position liquidatable but not yet worthless.\n        uint256 newPrice = bound(priceDrop, 1000e8, 1646e8);\n        ethFeed.setAnswer(int256(newPrice));\n        vm.assume(pool.isLiquidatable(alice));\n\n        repayAmount = bound(repayAmount, 1e6, 14_000e6);\n        uint256 collateralBefore = pool.collateralOf(alice);\n\n        vm.prank(keeper);\n        (uint256 repaid, uint256 seized) = pool.liquidate(alice, repayAmount, 0);\n\n        assertLe(repaid, 7_000e6, \"close factor holds\");\n        assertLe(seized, collateralBefore, \"cannot seize more than posted\");\n\n        uint256 repaidValue = repaid * 1e12; // USDC 6dp -> 18dp USD, USDC pegged at $1 here\n        uint256 seizedValue = seized * newPrice * 1e10 / 1e18;\n        assertLe(seizedValue, repaidValue * 105 / 100, \"bonus never exceeds 5%\");\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":439,"oldLines":7,"newStart":439,"newLines":8,"lines":["     }"," ","     function test_WithdrawReservesBoundedByIdleLiquidity() public {","- {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CdUU1zp3Wv3gfqXZ3UeqC","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01RYm9u8MKEDPjpqFEg4KWF2","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/security-goal-002/2026-07-28T122814Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/script/Deploy.s.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity 0.8.28;\n\nimport {Script, console2} from \"forge-std/Script.sol\";\nimport {IERC20Metadata} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/extensions/IERC20Metadata.sol\";\n\nimport {LendingPool} from \"../src/LendingPool.sol\";\nimport {IAggregatorV3} from \"../src/interfaces/IAggregatorV3.sol\";\n\n/// @notice Mainnet deployment for the WETH/USDC borrowing market.\n/// @dev Every address below MUST be re-verified against the canonical source before use —\n///      Etherscan for the tokens, docs.chain.link/data-feeds/price-feeds/addresses for the feeds.\n///      The script asserts each one on-chain as a second line of defence.\ncontract Deploy is Script {\n    address internal constant WETH = 0xC02aaA39b223FE8D0A0e5C4F27eAD9083C756Cc2;\n    address internal constant USDC = 0xA0b86991c6218b36c1d19D4a2e9Eb0cE3606eB48;\n    address internal constant ETH_USD_FEED = 0x5f4eC3Df9cbd43714FE2740f5E3616155c5b8419;\n    address internal constant USDC_USD_FEED = 0x8fFfFfd4AfB6115b954Bd326cbe7B4BA576818f6;\n\n    /// @dev ETH/USD publishes on a 1h heartbeat, USDC/USD on 24h. Windows add a margin on top\n    ///      so a slightly late round does not brick the market.\n    uint256 internal constant ETH_FEED_STALE_AFTER = 75 minutes;\n    uint256 internal constant USDC_FEED_STALE_AFTER = 26 hours;\n\n    uint256 internal constant BORROW_RATE_BPS = 500; // 5% APR\n    uint256 internal constant MIN_DEBT = 1_000e6; // 1,000 USDC — must stay worth liquidating\n\n    function run() external returns (LendingPool pool) {\n        // Owner must be a multisig / timelock, never the deployer EOA.\n        address owner = vm.envAddress(\"POOL_OWNER\");\n\n        _assertMainnetWiring();\n\n        vm.startBroadcast();\n        pool = new LendingPool(\n            owner,\n            WETH,\n            USDC,\n            ETH_USD_FEED,\n            USDC_USD_FEED,\n            BORROW_RATE_BPS,\n            MIN_DEBT,\n            ETH_FEED_STALE_AFTER,\n            USDC_FEED_STALE_AFTER\n        );\n        vm.stopBroadcast();\n\n        console2.log(\"LendingPool:\", address(pool));\n        console2.log(\"owner:\", owner);\n        console2.log(\"NEXT: fund() the pool with USDC, then verify the source on Etherscan.\");\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Fails the deploy if any wired address is not what we think it is.\n    function _assertMainnetWiring() internal view {\n        require(block.chainid == 1, \"not mainnet\");\n        require(IERC20Metadata(WETH).decimals() == 18, \"WETH decimals\");\n        require(IERC20Metadata(USDC).decimals() == 6, \"USDC decimals\");\n        require(_isDescription(ETH_USD_FEED, \"ETH / USD\"), \"ETH feed\");\n        require(_isDescription(USDC_USD_FEED, \"USDC / USD\"), \"USDC feed\");\n\n        (, int256 ethPrice,,, ) = IAggregatorV3(ETH_USD_FEED).latestRoundData();\n        require(ethPrice > 0, \"ETH feed answer\");\n        (, int256 usdcPrice,,, ) = IAggregatorV3(USDC_USD_FEED).latestRoundData();\n        require(usdcPrice > 0, \"USDC feed answer\");\n    }\n\n    function _isDescription(address feed, string memory expected) internal view returns (bool) {\n        return keccak256(bytes(IAggregatorV3(feed).description())) == keccak256(bytes(expected));\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":153,"cache_read_input_tokens":79324,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":153},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e022dfc2-ee4b-49d0-904f-ee7e007080f4","uuid":"1b6e8ac6-164d-40fb-b733-ca354642b557","timestamp":"2026-07-28T12:37:21.851Z","request_id":"req_011CdUU1txpRicuyeamE32iZ"}
{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01RYm9u8MKEDPjpqFEg4KWF2","type":"tool_result","content":"File created successfully at: /Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/security-goal-002/2026-07-28T122814Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/script/Deploy.s.sol (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e022dfc2-ee4b-49d0-904f-ee7e007080f4","uuid":"6aeda491-74eb-4048-a7e6-319c916d2d26","timestamp":"2026-07-28T12:37:21.873Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"create","filePath":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/security-goal-002/2026-07-28T122814Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/script/Deploy.s.sol","content":"// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity 0.8.28;\n\nimport {Script, console2} from \"forge-std/Script.sol\";\nimport {IERC20Metadata} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/extensions/IERC20Metadata.sol\";\n\nimport {LendingPool} from \"../src/LendingPool.sol\";\nimport {IAggregatorV3} from \"../src/interfaces/IAggregatorV3.sol\";\n\n/// @notice Mainnet deployment for the WETH/USDC borrowing market.\n/// @dev Every address below MUST be re-verified against the canonical source before use —\n///      Etherscan for the tokens, docs.chain.link/data-feeds/price-feeds/addresses for the feeds.\n///      The script asserts each one on-chain as a second line of defence.\ncontract Deploy is Script {\n    address internal constant WETH = 0xC02aaA39b223FE8D0A0e5C4F27eAD9083C756Cc2;\n    address internal constant USDC = 0xA0b86991c6218b36c1d19D4a2e9Eb0cE3606eB48;\n    address internal constant ETH_USD_FEED = 0x5f4eC3Df9cbd43714FE2740f5E3616155c5b8419;\n    address internal constant USDC_USD_FEED = 0x8fFfFfd4AfB6115b954Bd326cbe7B4BA576818f6;\n\n    /// @dev ETH/USD publishes on a 1h heartbeat, USDC/USD on 24h. Windows add a margin on top\n    ///      so a slightly late round does not brick the market.\n    uint256 internal constant ETH_FEED_STALE_AFTER = 75 minutes;\n    uint256 internal constant USDC_FEED_STALE_AFTER = 26 hours;\n\n    uint256 internal constant BORROW_RATE_BPS = 500; // 5% APR\n    uint256 internal constant MIN_DEBT = 1_000e6; // 1,000 USDC — must stay worth liquidating\n\n    function run() external returns (LendingPool pool) {\n        // Owner must be a multisig / timelock, never the deployer EOA.\n        address owner = vm.envAddress(\"POOL_OWNER\");\n\n        _assertMainnetWiring();\n\n        vm.startBroadcast();\n        pool = new LendingPool(\n            owner,\n            WETH,\n            USDC,\n            ETH_USD_FEED,\n            USDC_USD_FEED,\n            BORROW_RATE_BPS,\n            MIN_DEBT,\n            ETH_FEED_STALE_AFTER,\n            USDC_FEED_STALE_AFTER\n        );\n        vm.stopBroadcast();\n\n        console2.log(\"LendingPool:\", address(pool));\n        console2.log(\"owner:\", owner);\n        console2.log(\"NEXT: fund() the pool with USDC, then verify the source on Etherscan.\");\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Fails the deploy if any wired address is not what we think it is.\n    function _assertMainnetWiring() internal view {\n        require(block.chainid == 1, \"not mainnet\");\n        require(IERC20Metadata(WETH).decimals() == 18, \"WETH decimals\");\n        require(IERC20Metadata(USDC).decimals() == 6, \"USDC decimals\");\n        require(_isDescription(ETH_USD_FEED, \"ETH / USD\"), \"ETH feed\");\n        require(_isDescription(USDC_USD_FEED, \"USDC / USD\"), \"USDC feed\");\n\n        (, int256 ethPrice,,, ) = IAggregatorV3(ETH_USD_FEED).latestRoundData();\n        require(ethPrice > 0, \"ETH feed answer\");\n        (, int256 usdcPrice,,, ) = IAggregatorV3(USDC_USD_FEED).latestRoundData();\n        require(usdcPrice > 0, \"USDC feed answer\");\n    }\n\n    function _isDescription(address feed, string memory expected) internal view returns (bool) {\n        return keccak256(bytes(IAggregatorV3(feed).description())) == keccak256(bytes(expected));\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CdUU52M9s5hgL1DKRxWfD","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_017x4deUUoa3UAYdkSGvZP2T","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/security-goal-002/2026-07-28T122814Z-claude-with-skill-3/workspace/NOTES.md","content":"# WETH/USDC Borrowing Market — Notes\n\nSingle-pair, single-contract market: lock WETH, borrow USDC at up to 70% LTV, flat annual\ninterest, liquidatable above 85%, 5% liquidation bonus.\n\n- `src/LendingPool.sol` — the whole market.\n- `src/interfaces/IAggregatorV3.sol` — Chainlink read surface.\n- `script/Deploy.s.sol` — mainnet deployment with on-chain wiring assertions.\n- `test/` — 38 tests (unit + fuzz). `forge build`, `forge test`.\n\n## Scope, and one thing this is *not*\n\nThe task describes only the borrow side, so that is what this implements. **There is no public\nsupply side.** USDC liquidity is put in with `fund()` and only the owner can pull idle liquidity\nback out with `withdrawReserves()`. Interest accrues to the pool balance, i.e. to the operator.\n\nThat is a deliberate simplification, and it is also the single biggest thing to be aware of: if\nyou later want depositors to earn yield, you need supply-side shares (an ERC-4626-style\naccounting layer with a virtual-offset guard against the first-depositor inflation attack), and\n`withdrawReserves` has to go. Bolting that on afterwards is not a small change — decide before\ndeploying, not after.\n\n---\n\n## 1. How a position's health is computed\n\nEverything is priced into a common **18-decimal USD** unit. Token decimals are read from the\ntokens at construction (`collateralUnit`, `debtUnit`) — nothing assumes 18, because USDC is 6.\nFeed answers (8 decimals on both mainnet feeds) are lifted to 18 by an immutable scale factor.\n\n**Prices.** Both legs are priced from Chainlink. USDC is *not* assumed to be worth exactly $1 —\na depeg upward makes debt more expensive and can, correctly, make a position liquidatable.\nEvery read enforces: positive answer, complete round (`answeredInRound >= roundId`, non-zero\n`startedAt`/`updatedAt`), a timestamp that is not in the future, and an age under the per-feed\nstaleness window. Any failure reverts the whole action — the market freezes rather than\ntransacting on a bad price.\n\n**Values.**\n\n```\ncollateralValue = collateralAmount * ethPrice18 / 1e18       // WETH has 18 decimals\ndebtValue       = debtAmount       * usdcPrice18 / 1e6        // USDC has 6, rounded UP\n```\n\n**Debt.** Interest uses a RAY-scaled (1e27) cumulative index, Aave-style. A position stores\nindex-scaled debt; actual debt is `scaledDebt * borrowIndex / RAY`, rounded **up**.\n\n```\naccrue():  borrowIndex *= (1 + rateBps * elapsed / (10_000 * 365 days))\n```\n\n`accrue()` is public and runs at the top of every state-changing entry point, so health is never\nevaluated against a stale index. If nothing is borrowed, the clock advances but the index does\nnot — idle time can never be charged retroactively. Rate changes accrue at the *old* rate first.\n\nThe rate is flat, as specified: simple interest inside an accrual window, compounding across\nwindows. Frequent accrual therefore nudges the effective rate slightly above the nominal APR\n(5% APR → at most ~5.13% if accrued every block). That drift is bounded, always in the pool's\nfavour, and it is the standard behaviour of this index design.\n\n**The two thresholds.**\n\n```\nmaxDebtValue      = collateralValue * 7000 / 10_000     // borrow / withdraw limit\nliquidationValue  = collateralValue * 8500 / 10_000     // liquidation trigger\n```\n\n- **Borrow or withdraw** is allowed only while `debtValue <= maxDebtValue` (70%). Withdrawals are\n  checked against the 70% line, not the 85% one, so no one can walk their own position to the\n  edge of liquidation in a single transaction.\n- **Liquidatable** when `debtValue > liquidationValue` (85%). Strictly greater — exactly 85% is\n  still safe.\n- `healthFactor(account)` returns `liquidationValue * 1e18 / debtValue`; below `1e18` is\n  liquidatable, `type(uint256).max` when there is no debt.\n\nThe 15-point gap between 70% and 85% is the buffer that absorbs price moves and accrued interest.\nA position at exactly 70% LTV with a 5% rate and a flat ETH price becomes liquidatable after\nroughly four years of interest alone.\n\n**Rounding is always in the pool's favour**: debt rounds up, collateral value and liquidator\npayouts round down, scaled debt rounds up on borrow and down on repay. Full repayment burns the\nexact stored scaled balance so rounding cannot leave un-repayable dust.\n\n`positionOf()` returns collateral, debt, and all four USD figures in one call for a keeper or UI.\n\n---\n\n## 2. What a liquidator has to do\n\n**Find one.** `isLiquidatable(borrower)` or `healthFactor(borrower) < 1e18`. Positions become\nliquidatable from ETH falling, USDC appreciating, or interest accruing — watch all three.\nNote that both views revert if a feed is stale or unhealthy; treat a revert as \"cannot evaluate\",\nnot \"healthy\".\n\n**Size it.** `previewLiquidation(borrower, repayAmount)` returns exactly what `liquidate` would\ndo — same internal code path, so it cannot drift from the real thing. Pass\n`type(uint256).max` to get the largest allowed liquidation.\n\n**Do it.**\n\n```solidity\nusdc.approve(pool, repaid);                              // exact amount, not type(uint256).max\npool.liquidate(borrower, repayAmount, minCollateralOut); // pull USDC, receive WETH\n```\n\nThe rules the contract enforces:\n\n- **Close factor 50%.** At most half a position's debt per call. The cap is lifted to 100% when\n  half would leave a remainder under `minDebt`, so positions never rot into unliquidatable dust.\n  Repeat calls are fine — each one re-evaluates health.\n- **Bonus 5%.** You receive collateral worth `repaidValue * 1.05`, rounded down:\n  `seize = (repayAmount * usdcPrice * 1.05) / ethPrice`, decimal-adjusted.\n- **Underwater positions.** If the bonus would exceed the collateral posted, you get *all* the\n  remaining collateral and your repayment is scaled **down** to match (`collateralValue / 1.05`)\n  — you are never made to overpay for a short position. The leftover debt becomes bad debt with\n  zero collateral behind it; `BadDebt` is emitted. Nobody has an incentive to clear that\n  remainder, so it sits on the pool.\n- **`minCollateralOut` is your slippage guard.** It is not optional in practice. The seize amount\n  is computed from the oracle price *at execution time*; a price move between simulation and\n  inclusion changes your payout. Set it, and send liquidations through a private mempool\n  (Flashbots Protect) — liquidations are prime MEV targets and a public tx will be backrun or\n  outright stolen.\n- You receive **WETH, not ETH**. Unwrap and sell separately; the pool does no swapping, so your\n  price risk between seizing and selling is yours.\n- Liquidations are blocked while the pool is paused. See below — that is a real risk to price in.\n\n---\n\n## 3. What an operator has to get right on mainnet\n\n**Before deploying**\n\n- [ ] **Verify every address.** `script/Deploy.s.sol` hardcodes WETH, USDC, and the two Chainlink\n      feeds, and asserts `decimals()` and feed `description()` on-chain before deploying. Still\n      check each against Etherscan and `docs.chain.link` by hand. A wrong feed address is a total\n      loss, and the assertion only proves the contract *claims* to be ETH/USD.\n- [ ] **`POOL_OWNER` must be a multisig or timelock.** Never the deployer EOA. Ownership is\n      `Ownable2Step`, so the new owner must call `acceptOwnership()` — do not skip that handshake.\n- [ ] **Match staleness windows to the feeds' real heartbeats.** ETH/USD is 1h, USDC/USD is 24h;\n      the script uses 75 min and 26 h. Too tight bricks the market on a late round; too loose lets\n      the market trade on a dead price. Re-check whenever Chainlink changes a feed's parameters,\n      and use `setStalenessWindows()` to adjust.\n- [ ] **Set `minDebt` against real gas costs.** 1,000 USDC by default. Below that, a liquidation\n      may cost more in gas than the 5% bonus pays, and positions stop being cleared.\n- [ ] **Run static analysis.** Slither and Mythril were **not** run here — neither is installed in\n      this environment. Run `slither .` and `forge test --fuzz-runs 10000` before deploying, and\n      resolve everything in the reentrancy / unchecked-return / unprotected-function categories.\n- [ ] **Get it audited.** This is unaudited code holding other people's money.\n\n**At deploy**\n\n- [ ] `fund()` the pool with USDC. There is no borrow cap — the funded balance *is* the cap, so\n      fund only what you can afford to have lent out, and top up as demand proves itself.\n- [ ] Verify the source on Etherscan (`forge verify-contract`) immediately.\n- [ ] Confirm on-chain that `owner()`, `borrowRateBps`, `minDebt`, and both staleness windows read\n      back as intended before announcing the market.\n\n**Running it**\n\n- [ ] **Have liquidators before you have borrowers.** The market's solvency depends entirely on\n      somebody calling `liquidate()` promptly. Run your own keeper as a backstop from day one and\n      alert on `healthFactor < 1.05e18`. The 5% bonus is thin during volatile blocks.\n- [ ] **Watch `BadDebt` events.** Each one is an unrecoverable loss to the pool. A cluster means\n      the 85% threshold or the 5% bonus is mispriced for how fast ETH is moving.\n- [ ] **`setBorrowRate` is capped at 50% APR** and accrues at the old rate first, so a rate change\n      cannot be applied retroactively. It still changes the terms under existing borrowers with no\n      notice — announce changes, and consider routing them through a timelock.\n- [ ] **Pause is a loaded gun.** `pause()` blocks deposits, borrows, withdrawals *and\n      liquidations*; only `repay()` stays open so borrowers can always de-risk. A single key that\n      can freeze users' collateral is a censorship and liveness risk, and pausing during a crash\n      stops liquidations exactly when they matter. Use it only for oracle failure or a discovered\n      bug, keep it behind the multisig, and have a written policy for when it is used.\n- [ ] **`withdrawReserves` is a trust assumption, not a bug.** The owner can withdraw all idle\n      USDC at any time. Borrowers' collateral is untouchable, but anyone lending liquidity is\n      trusting the owner completely. Say so publicly.\n\n**Known limitations / accepted risks**\n\n- **Single oracle, no fallback.** If Chainlink stops publishing ETH/USD, every action except\n  `repay()` reverts until it resumes. That is the intended failure mode (freeze, don't guess), but\n  it means an extended feed outage during a crash prevents liquidations and can produce bad debt.\n  A secondary feed or a Uniswap V3 TWAP as a sanity band is the natural next hardening step.\n- **Chainlink circuit breakers.** Some feeds have `minAnswer`/`maxAnswer` bounds; if ETH's price\n  ever exited them the feed would report the bound, not the truth. Nothing on-chain can detect\n  this — monitor off-chain.\n- **USDC is upgradeable and has a blocklist.** Circle can pause transfers or block addresses. A\n  blocked liquidator's call reverts; if the *pool* were blocked, only accounting would keep\n  working. This risk is inherited and cannot be engineered away.\n- **Bad debt is not socialised or auctioned.** It simply sits on the pool as a loss.\n- **No ETH entry point.** Borrowers must wrap to WETH themselves.\n- **Interest goes to the pool balance**, not to any depositor accounting — see the scope note.\n\n**Security properties that are implemented** (each with a test): CEI ordering plus\n`nonReentrant` on every external-call path, `SafeERC20` throughout, balance-delta accounting on\ncollateral deposits, decimals read from tokens rather than assumed, multiply-before-divide with\nexplicit rounding directions, full Chainlink validation, zero-address/zero-amount validation,\nevents on every state change, exact-amount approvals in the liquidator flow, a close factor, a\ndust floor, and a slippage guard on liquidation.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":317,"cache_read_input_tokens":81959,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":317},"output_tokens":58,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e022dfc2-ee4b-49d0-904f-ee7e007080f4","uuid":"2292d869-0713-472c-8000-fed154b8f4d3","timestamp":"2026-07-28T12:38:34.624Z","request_id":"req_011CdUU51K8zmZV2vxtGi62v"}

A single key that\n      can freeze users' collateral is a censorship and liveness risk, and pausing during a crash\n      stops liquidations exactly when they matter. Use it only for oracle failure or a discovered\n      bug, keep it behind the multisig, and have a written policy for when it is used.\n- [ ] **`withdrawReserves` is a trust assumption, not a bug.** The owner can withdraw all idle\n      USDC at any time. Borrowers' collateral is untouchable, but anyone lending liquidity is\n      trusting the owner completely. Say so publicly.\n\n**Known limitations / accepted risks**\n\n- **Single oracle, no fallback.** If Chainlink stops publishing ETH/USD, every action except\n  `repay()` reverts until it resumes. That is the intended failure mode (freeze, don't guess), but\n  it means an extended feed outage during a crash prevents liquidations and can produce bad debt.\n  A secondary feed or a Uniswap V3 TWAP as a sanity band is the natural next hardening step.\n- **Chainlink circuit breakers.** Some feeds have `minAnswer`/`maxAnswer` bounds; if ETH's price\n  ever exited them the feed would report the bound, not the truth. Nothing on-chain can detect\n  this — monitor off-chain.\n- **USDC is upgradeable and has a blocklist.** Circle can pause transfers or block addresses. A\n  blocked liquidator's call reverts; if the *pool* were blocked, only accounting would keep\n  working. This risk is inherited and cannot be engineered away.\n- **Bad debt is not socialised or auctioned.** It simply sits on the pool as a loss.\n- **No ETH entry point.** Borrowers must wrap to WETH themselves.\n- **Interest goes to the pool balance**, not to any depositor accounting — see the scope note.\n\n**Security properties that are implemented** (each with a test): CEI ordering plus\n`nonReentrant` on every external-call path, `SafeERC20` throughout, balance-delta accounting on\ncollateral deposits, decimals read from tokens rather than assumed, multiply-before-divide with\nexplicit rounding directions, full Chainlink validation, zero-address/zero-amount validation,\nevents on every state change, exact-amount approvals in the liquidator flow, a close factor, a\ndust floor, and a slippage guard on liquidation.\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CdUUCPGBuuLSSU73T88Pf","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Done. Foundry project builds clean and all 38 tests pass.\n\n**`src/LendingPool.sol`** — the whole market in one contract:\n\n- **Health**: everything priced into 18-decimal USD via Chainlink ETH/USD *and* USDC/USD (USDC is not assumed to be $1 — a depeg is priced in). Token decimals are read at construction, so the 6-dp USDC path is real, not assumed. Borrow/withdraw gated at 70%; liquidatable above 85%.\n- **Interest**: RAY-scaled cumulative index, accrued at the top of every entry point. Idle time can't be charged retroactively; rate changes accrue at the old rate first.\n- **Liquidation**: 50% close factor (lifted to 100% when the remainder would be dust), 5% bonus, `minCollateralOut` slippage guard, and an underwater path that seizes all collateral and *scales the repayment down* so the liquidator isn't made to overpay — leftover is emitted as `BadDebt`.\n- **Defenses**: CEI + `nonReentrant` everywhere, SafeERC20, balance-delta accounting on deposits, full Chainlink validation (positive answer, complete round, non-future timestamp, bounded age), multiply-before-divide with rounding always toward the pool, `Ownable2Step`, events on every state change.\n\n**`script/Deploy.s.sol`** asserts its hardcoded mainnet addresses on-chain (token decimals, feed `description()`, live answers) before deploying, and requires `POOL_OWNER` from env.\n\n**`NOTES.md`** covers health math, the liquidator playbook, and the operator checklist.\n\nThree things worth your attention:\n\n1. **No public supply side.** The task describes only the borrow side, so USDC liquidity comes from `fund()` and only the owner can pull idle liquidity back. That means anyone funding the pool trusts the owner completely, and interest accrues to the operator. If you want lender shares, decide before deploying — retrofitting means real accounting changes (and an inflation-attack guard).\n2. **Slither/Mythril were not run** — neither is installed here. The checklist item is genuinely open; NOTES flags it.\n3. `pause()` blocks liquidations too, which is the standard tradeoff but stops the safety mechanism exactly when it matters. Documented, but it's a policy decision, not a code one.\n\nI did not send the feedback note the skill asks for at the end — that's a post to an external service, so I'd rather you confirm before I do it."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":556,"cache_read_input_tokens":97170,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":556},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e022dfc2-ee4b-49d0-904f-ee7e007080f4","uuid":"0ef6c819-0943-4d65-a65a-2b0afb2477b5","timestamp":"2026-07-28T12:39:24.204Z","request_id":"req_011CdUUCM2k6Hch7x7NPdG2T"}
